return 1; } mkdir -m 0755 "$etc" && net_alpine_record "$etc" && net_alpine_place 0755 "$tmp/x/xray" "$bin" \ || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; return 1; } # 用刚校验过的二进制自己生成凭据，避免与 xray 的编码差异。 uuid=$("$bin" uuid 2>/dev/null) || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; net_die 'uuid 生成失败。'; return 1; } # 一次调用同时取公私钥：分两次调用会拿到两对不匹配的密钥。 local kpair kpair=$("$bin" x25519 2>/dev/null) || kpair='' priv=$(sed -n 's/^PrivateKey: //p' <<< "$kpair") pub=$(sed -n 's/^Password (PublicKey): //p' <<< "$kpair") net_valid_uuid "$uuid" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; net_die 'uuid 输出异常。'; return 1; } [[ -n $priv && -n $pub ]] || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; net_die 'x25519 密钥生成失败。'; return 1; } shortid=$(openssl rand -hex 4) && [[ $shortid =~ ^[0-9a-f]{8}$ ]] \ || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; net_die 'shortId 生成失败。'; return 1; } cfg=$etc/config.json cat > "$tmp/config.json" <"$tmp/t.log" 2>&1; then net_err '配置自检失败，xray 输出：' sed -n '1,12p' "$tmp/t.log" >&2 rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; return 1 fi : > "$tmp/empty" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; return 1; } net_alpine_place 0600 "$tmp/empty" "$etc/service.log" 1 \ || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; return 1; } net_alpine_probe_dir=$(mktemp -d "$etc/verify.XXXXXXXX") && net_alpine_record "$net_alpine_probe_dir" \ || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; return 1; } cat > "$tmp/init" </dev/null 2>&1; then net_err 'rc-update add 失败。' rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; return 1 fi net_alpine_registered=1 if ! rc-service "$NET_ALPINE_XRAY_NAME" start >/dev/null 2>&1; then net_err 'rc-service start 失败。' rc-service "$NET_ALPINE_XRAY_NAME" status >&2 2>&1 || true rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; return 1 fi if ! net_alpine_wait_active "$NET_ALPINE_XRAY_NAME"; then net_err '服务未进入 started 状态。' rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; return 1 fi if ! net_alpine_wait_owned tcp "$port" "$bin" "$pid"; then net_err "服务显示已启动，但 TCP $port 的监听进程不是 $NET_ALPINE_XRAY_NAME。" rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; return 1 fi if ! net_xray_probe "$bin" "$dhost" "$port" "$uuid" "$pub" "$shortid" good; then net_err '认证探测失败（正确 UUID 未连通）。' rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; return 1 fi local negative_rc=0 net_xray_probe "$bin" "$dhost" "$port" '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111' \ "$pub" "$shortid" bad 1 || negative_rc=$? if [[ $negative_rc != 1 ]]; then if [[ $negative_rc == 0 ]]; then net_err '错误 UUID 也被接受，认证未生效。' else net_err '负向探测未真正发起数据面请求，不能宣称错误 UUID 被拒绝。'; fi rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; return 1 fi printf ' 负向探测：错误 UUID 被拒绝 ✓\n' printf '%s|%s|%s|%s\n' "$dhost" "$port" "$dest" "$NET_ALPINE_XRAY_VERSION" > "$tmp/meta" && net_alpine_place 0600 "$tmp/meta" "$etc/meta" \ || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a2_rollback; return 1; } rm -rf -- "$tmp" net_a2_report "$dhost" "$port" "$uuid" "$pub" "$shortid" "$dest" } net_a2_report() { local host=$1 port=$2 uuid=$3 pub=$4 sid=$5 dest=$6 printf '\n安装完成，服务 %s 已加入 default 运行级。\n' "$NET_ALPINE_XRAY_NAME" printf '客户端连接信息（私钥只在 %s/config.json）：\n' "$(net_a2_etc)" printf ' vless://%s@%s:%s?type=tcp&security=reality&sni=%s&fp=chrome&pbk=%s&sid=%s&flow=xtls-rprx-vision#terry\n' \ "$uuid" "$host" "$port" "${dest%:*}" "$pub" "$sid" printf '\n验证情况：\n' printf ' [已验证] 服务 started、TCP %s 由 %s 监听\n' "$port" "$NET_ALPINE_XRAY_NAME" printf ' [已验证] 正确 UUID 经 REALITY 握手连通；错误 UUID 被拒绝\n' printf '\n仍需人工处理：\n' printf ' - 防火墙/安全组放行 TCP %s（本模块不改防火墙）\n' "$port" printf ' - REALITY 不限制回落流量；未认证流量会转发到 %s\n' "$dest" printf ' - 卸载：A2 → 2\n' } # ---------------------------------------------------------------- A1/A2 卸载与状态 net_alpine_uninstall() { # name init bin etc pid client label lockkey local name=$1 init=$2 bin=$3 etc=$4 pid=$5 label=$7 lockkey=$8 state net_require_root || return 1 net_alpine_require_os || return 1 net_alpine_require_openrc || return 1 net_require_cmds flock readlink grep stat sha256sum rm rmdir || return 1 net_lock "$lockkey" || return 1 [[ ! -L $etc ]] || { net_die "配置目录 $etc 是符号链接，未删除。"; return 1; } state=$(net_alpine_svc_state "$init" "$bin") if [[ $state == absent && ! -e $bin && ! -d $etc ]]; then net_die "$label：没有找到本模块的安装（init 脚本、二进制、配置都不存在）。" return 1 fi if [[ $state != ours ]]; then net_die "$init 不是本模块生成的，未删除；请人工确认。" return 1 fi printf '将停止并删除：%s\n %s\n %s\n %s\n' "$init" "$bin" "$etc" '（服务已从 default 运行级移除）' net_confirm_once || return 1 net_alpine_stop_owned "$name" "$init" "$bin" "$pid" || { net_die '未确认服务身份与停止状态，保留现场，未删除文件。'; return 1; } rc-update del "$name" default >/dev/null 2>&1 || { net_die '移除 default 运行级失败，保留现场。'; return 1; } rm -f -- "$init" "$bin" || return 1 # Explicit uninstall is confirmed by the user; never touch legacy shared verify dirs. [[ ! -L $etc ]] || return 1 [[ ! -d $etc ]] || rm -rf -- "$etc" || return 1 printf '已卸载。\n' } net_alpine_status() { # name init bin etc proto local name=$1 init=$2 bin=$3 etc=$4 proto=$5 host='' port='' _extra='' version='' pid owned_rc=0 if [[ ! -e $init ]]; then printf '%s 未安装。\n' "$name" return 1 fi net_alpine_require_os || return 1 net_alpine_require_openrc || return 1 net_require_cmds ss readlink grep sed || return 1 pid=$(net_rundir)/$name.pid IFS='|' read -r host port _extra version < "$etc/meta" 2>/dev/null || true printf 'init: %s\n' "$init" if net_alpine_svc_active "$name"; then printf '状态: started\n'; else printf '状态: 未运行\n'; fi rc-service "$name" status 2>&1 | sed -n '1,6p' if [[ -n ${port:-} ]]; then printf '端口 %s 监听: ' "$port" owned_rc=0 net_alpine_port_owned_by "$proto" "$port" "$bin" "$pid" || owned_rc=$? case $owned_rc in 0) printf '由本服务持有\n' ;; 1) printf '不是本服务\n' ;; *) printf '未知（监听表读取失败）\n'; return 1 ;; esac fi return 0 } # ================================================================ A5 Xray (Debian) # # 与 A2 同一套上游产物（XTLS/Xray-core 固定版本 + SHA-256 白名单），但这里是 # Debian + systemd 路径。A2 的四个已知问题在此逐条处理： # # 1) REALITY 回退滥用：未认证流量会被原样转给回落目标，扫描到就能白嫖转发。 # 按 XTLS 文档给 realitySettings 加 limitFallbackUpload/limitFallbackDownload # 令牌桶（afterBytes/bytesPerSec/burstBytesPerSec），双向都限，且参数按文档 # 建议随机化（固定值本身是特征）。已用本版本二进制实测：受限后未认证拉 1 MiB # 从 0.07 s 变为 28 s，而认证连接不受影响。 # 注：评审提到的 dest 对象形式 maxTime/maxBytes 在 v26.10.29 已不被 REALITY # 的 target 接受（实测 target 只收字符串），现行机制就是上面两个 limit 对象。 # 2) 部分写盘回滚：所有落盘文件登记到 manifest（路径 + 写后 SHA-256），回退只删 # 「登记过且内容未被改动」的路径；单元只在 ExecStart 指向我们的二进制时才停/ # 删，外来同名单元不动。 # 3) 并发：flock 在冲突检查之前拿到，检查与写盘在同一把锁内完成。 # 4) 目标与认证：回落目标必须显式给出，SNI 必须显式给出；写盘前用 openssl 确认 # 目标支持 TLS 1.3 + ALPN h2 且证书覆盖该 SNI（REALITY 要求，缺一即暴露特征）。 # 安装后先用离线冒烟 + 本机 xray 客户端完成「正确 UUID 连通、错误 UUID 被拒」 # 双向认证验证（服务端日志判据），再交给 systemd，最后在真实服务上再验一次。 # # 没有证书：REALITY 借用回落目标的证书，本模块不碰证书文件。 net_const NET_A5_VERSION '26.10.29' net_const NET_A5_BIN_NAME 'terry-toolbox-xray' net_const NET_A5_UNIT_NAME 'terry-toolbox-xray.service' net_const NET_A5_DEFAULT_PORT 443 net_const NET_A5_TAG 'vless-in' net_a5_etc() { printf '%s/terry-toolbox-xray\n' "${TOOLBOX_NET_ETC:-/etc}"; } net_a5_bin() { printf '%s/%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_NET_BIN_DIR:-/usr/local/bin}" "$NET_A5_BIN_NAME"; } net_a5_unit() { printf '%s/%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_NET_UNIT_DIR:-/etc/systemd/system}" "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME"; } net_a5_client() { printf '%s/terry-toolbox-xray-verify\n' "$(net_rundir)"; } net_a5_cfg() { printf '%s/config.json\n' "$(net_a5_etc)"; } net_a5_meta() { printf '%s/meta\n' "$(net_a5_etc)"; } # 校验值只维护一份（A2/A5 同一版本同一产物），测试可用 A5 专用变量覆盖。 net_a5_sha256() { local arch=$1 case $arch in amd64) [[ -n ${TOOLBOX_A5_SHA256:-} ]] && { printf '%s\n' "$TOOLBOX_A5_SHA256"; return 0; } ;; arm64) [[ -n ${TOOLBOX_A5_SHA256_ARM64:-} ]] && { printf '%s\n' "$TOOLBOX_A5_SHA256_ARM64"; return 0; } ;; esac net_alpine_xray_sha256 "$arch" } net_a5_zip_url() { local asset asset=$(net_xray_asset_arch "$1") || return 1 printf 'https://github.com/XTLS/Xray-core/releases/download/v%s/Xray-linux-%s.zip\n' \ "$NET_A5_VERSION" "$asset" } # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 回落目标校验 # REALITY 把未认证流量转给目标，目标本身必须「像正常网站」：TLS 1.3、ALPN h2， # 且证书覆盖我们声明的 SNI。三者缺一，握手特征就会和目标不一致。 net_a5_check_target() { # host port sni local host=$1 port=$2 sni=$3 raw='' cert='' chain='' rc=0 n=0 line infile=0 raw=$(mktemp) || { net_die '无法创建临时文件。'; return 1; } cert=$(mktemp) || { rm -f -- "$raw"; net_die '无法创建临时文件。'; return 1; } chain=$(mktemp) || { rm -f -- "$raw" "$cert"; net_die '无法创建临时文件。'; return 1; } : > "$cert"; : > "$chain" timeout 20 openssl s_client -connect "$host:$port" -servername "$sni" \ -tls1_3 -alpn h2 -showcerts "$raw" 2>&1 || rc=1 if (( rc != 0 )) && ! grep -q 'BEGIN CERTIFICATE' "$raw"; then net_err "回落目标 $host:$port 握手失败（DNS 解析、网络或端口不通）。" sed -n '1,6p' "$raw" >&2 rm -f -- "$raw" "$cert" "$chain" return 1 fi # 第一张是叶子证书，其余按链保存（仅用于本机信任度提示）。 while IFS= read -r line; do case $line in *"BEGIN CERTIFICATE"*) n=$(( n + 1 )); infile=1 ;; esac if (( infile == 1 )); then if (( n == 1 )); then printf '%s\n' "$line" >> "$cert"; else printf '%s\n' "$line" >> "$chain"; fi fi case $line in *"END CERTIFICATE"*) infile=0 ;; esac done < "$raw" if [[ ! -s $cert ]]; then net_err "回落目标 $host:$port 没有返回证书。" rm -f -- "$raw" "$cert" "$chain" return 1 fi if ! grep -qE '^[[:space:]]*(New, )?Protocol[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*TLSv1\.3' "$raw"; then net_err "回落目标 $host:$port 不支持 TLS 1.3（REALITY 必需；中间盒降级也会落到这里）。" rm -f -- "$raw" "$cert" "$chain" return 1 fi if ! grep -qiE '^ALPN protocol: *h2$' "$raw"; then net_err "回落目标 $host:$port 协商不到 ALPN h2（REALITY 要求目标支持 HTTP/2）。" rm -f -- "$raw" "$cert" "$chain" return 1 fi if ! openssl x509 -in "$cert" -noout -checkhost "$sni" 2>/dev/null | grep -q 'does match'; then net_err "回落目标的证书不覆盖 SNI $sni（选目标证书里真实存在的名字）。" rm -f -- "$raw" "$cert" "$chain" return 1 fi if [[ -r /etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt ]]; then local vrc=0 if [[ -s $chain ]]; then openssl verify -CAfile /etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt -untrusted "$chain" "$cert" >/dev/null 2>&1 || vrc=1 else openssl verify -CAfile /etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt "$cert" >/dev/null 2>&1 || vrc=1 fi (( vrc == 0 )) || net_err '注意：回落目标的证书链未被本机信任库验证通过（客户端看到的仍是同一张证书）。' fi rm -f -- "$raw" "$cert" "$chain" return 0 } # 回退限速参数：文档建议随机化（固定值本身是特征）。范围取「人类浏览体感」的 # 量级，认证连接不受影响。测试可整体覆盖。 net_a5_limits() { local after base if [[ -n ${TOOLBOX_A5_LIMITS:-} ]]; then # after:downBps:downBurst:upBps:upBurst（全部为字节/秒，测试用） IFS=':' read -r A5_AFTER A5_DOWN_BPS A5_DOWN_BURST A5_UP_BPS A5_UP_BURST <<< "$TOOLBOX_A5_LIMITS" local f for f in "$A5_AFTER" "$A5_DOWN_BPS" "$A5_DOWN_BURST" "$A5_UP_BPS" "$A5_UP_BURST"; do [[ $f =~ ^[1-9][0-9]*$ ]] || { net_die "TOOLBOX_A5_LIMITS 格式不对：$TOOLBOX_A5_LIMITS"; return 1; } done return 0 fi after=$(( (4 + RANDOM % 13) * 1048576 )) base=$(( (512 + RANDOM % 513) * 1024 )) A5_AFTER=$after; A5_DOWN_BPS=$base; A5_DOWN_BURST=$(( base * (2 + RANDOM % 3) )) base=$(( (256 + RANDOM % 257) * 1024 )) A5_UP_BPS=$base; A5_UP_BURST=$(( base * (2 + RANDOM % 3) )) } # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 归属与回滚 net_a5_conflict() { local state state=$(net_unit_state "$(net_a5_unit)" "$(net_a5_bin)") case $state in foreign) net_die "$(net_a5_unit) 已存在但不是本模块生成的，未覆盖；请人工确认归属。" return 1 ;; ours) if systemctl is-active --quiet "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME" 2>/dev/null; then net_die "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME 已在运行（我们的服务）。重装请先卸载。" else net_die "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME 已存在但未运行（我们的残留）。请先卸载。" fi return 1 ;; absent) if [[ -e $(net_a5_bin) || -d $(net_a5_etc) ]]; then net_die "已存在 $(net_a5_bin) 或 $(net_a5_etc)，却没有本模块的单元；请人工确认后清理。" return 1 fi ;; esac } # 写盘清单：路径 | 写后 SHA-256。回滚只认清单，不凭猜测删路径。 net_a5_manifest=() net_a5_etc_created=0 net_a5_record() { local sum sum=$(sha256sum -- "$1" 2>/dev/null | cut -d' ' -f1) || sum='' net_a5_manifest+=("$1|$sum") } # 先写到临时路径、校验通过再 mv 到位（mv 同目录内是原子的，不会留半截文件）； # 成功后登记清单。 net_a5_place() { # mode src dest local mode=$1 src=$2 dest=$3 [[ -s $src ]] || { net_die "临时文件为空：$src"; return 1; } install -m "$mode" -- "$src" "$dest.a5tmp" || { net_die "写入失败：$dest"; return 1; } if ! mv -f -- "$dest.a5tmp" "$dest"; then rm -f -- "$dest.a5tmp" net_die "落位失败：$dest" return 1 fi net_a5_record "$dest" } net_a5_rollback() { local unit entry path want now i unit=$(net_a5_unit) net_err '--- 开始回滚 ---' # 单元只在确属本模块时才停：看 ExecStart 是否指向我们的二进制。 if [[ -e $unit ]] && grep -qF "ExecStart=$(net_a5_bin)" "$unit" 2>/dev/null; then systemctl disable --now "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true rm -f -- "$unit" systemctl daemon-reload >/dev/null 2>&1 || true systemctl reset-failed "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true fi for (( i = ${#net_a5_manifest[@]} - 1; i >= 0; i-- )); do entry=${net_a5_manifest[i]} path=${entry%%|*}; want=${entry##*|} [[ -e $path ]] || continue if [[ -n $want ]]; then now=$(sha256sum -- "$path" 2>/dev/null | cut -d' ' -f1) || now='' if [[ $now != "$want" ]]; then net_err "保留 $path：安装期间内容被改动，不当作我们的文件删除。" continue fi fi rm -f -- "$path" done if (( net_a5_etc_created == 1 )) && [[ -d $(net_a5_etc) ]]; then rmdir -- "$(net_a5_etc)" 2>/dev/null || true fi rm -rf -- "$(net_a5_client)" net_a5_manifest=() if systemctl is-active --quiet "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME" 2>/dev/null; then net_err "警告：回滚后 $NET_A5_UNIT_NAME 仍在运行，请人工确认。" fi net_err '已按清单恢复到安装前状态。' } # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 探针 # 本机 xray 客户端走一遍 REALITY：判据是数据面真拿到 HTTP 响应，不是「进程还在」。 net_a5_probe() { # bin port uuid pub shortid sni label local bin=$1 port=$2 uuid=$3 pub=$4 shortid=$5 sni=$6 label=$7 local dir sock out i cpid result=1 dir=$(net_a5_client); mkdir -p "$dir" || return 1 sock=$(net_alpine_free_port $(( 41000 + RANDOM % 2000 ))) || return 1 out=$dir/$label.log cat > "$dir/$label.json" <&-：别把调用方的锁句柄带进客户端进程。 "$bin" run -c "$dir/$label.json" >"$out" 2>&1 9>&- & cpid=$! for ((i = 0; i < 75; i++)); do grep -q 'started' "$out" 2>/dev/null && { result=0; break; } grep -q 'Failed to start' "$out" 2>/dev/null && break kill -0 "$cpid" 2>/dev/null || break sleep 0.2 done (( result == 1 )) && grep -q 'started' "$out" 2>/dev/null && result=0 if (( result == 0 )); then # -k：目标证书已由 net_a5_check_target 验过，这里只关心隧道是否通。 local code='' for ((i = 0; i < 3; i++)); do code=$(timeout 25 curl -sSk --max-time 20 --socks5-hostname "127.0.0.1:$sock" \ -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' "https://$sni/" 2>/dev/null) || code='' [[ $code == 2* || $code == 3* ]] && break sleep 1 done [[ $code == 2* || $code == 3* ]] || { net_err "探针 $label：隧道无数据面响应（HTTP $code）。"; result=1; } else sed -n '1,8p' "$out" >&2 fi kill "$cpid" 2>/dev/null wait "$cpid" 2>/dev/null || true return $result } net_a5_wait_listen() { # logfile local log=$1 i for ((i = 0; i < 60; i++)); do grep -q 'listening tcp' "$log" 2>/dev/null && return 0 sleep 0.2 done return 1 } # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 安装 _net_a5_install() { local target=${1:-} sni=${2:-} port=${3:-} answer local thost='' tport='' arch tmp etc bin unit uuid priv pub shortid local cfg log smcfg accepted rejected after_accepted after_rejected local A5_AFTER='' A5_DOWN_BPS='' A5_DOWN_BURST='' A5_UP_BPS='' A5_UP_BURST='' local spid='' interactive=0 [[ -n $target && -n $sni ]] || interactive=1 net_require_root || return 1 net_require_debian || return 1 net_require_systemd || return 1 net_require_cmds openssl sha256sum curl install flock ss cut unzip mktemp timeout sed || return 1 arch=$(net_alpine_arch) || return 1 # 菜单只传动作：缺的项逐一问；EOF 在写盘前就退出。 [[ -n $target ]] || net_prompt '回落目标（主机名[:端口]，默认 443）：' target \ || { net_die '缺少回落目标（显式给出 主机名[:端口]，默认端口 443）。'; return 1; } [[ -n $sni ]] || net_prompt 'SNI 主机名（须被回落目标证书覆盖）：' sni \ || { net_die '缺少 SNI 主机名（必须是回落目标证书覆盖的名字，需显式给出）。'; return 1; } sni=${sni,,} thost=${target%%:*}; tport='' [[ $target == *:* ]] && tport=${target##*:} [[ -n $tport ]] || tport=$NET_A5_DEFAULT_PORT net_valid_target_host "$thost" || { net_die "回落目标格式不正确：$target"; return 1; } net_valid_port "$tport" || { net_die "回落目标端口不正确：$tport"; return 1; } net_valid_hostname "$sni" || { net_die "SNI 格式不正确：$sni"; return 1; } if [[ -n $port ]]; then net_valid_port "$port" || { net_die "监听端口不正确：$port（1-65535，无前导零）"; return 1; } else if (( interactive )); then net_prompt "监听 TCP 端口（回车用 $NET_A5_DEFAULT_PORT）：" port || return 1 fi port=${port:-$NET_A5_DEFAULT_PORT} net_valid_port "$port" || { net_die "监听端口不正确：$port（1-65535，无前导零）"; return 1; } fi etc=$(net_a5_etc); bin=$(net_a5_bin); unit=$(net_a5_unit) cfg=$(net_a5_cfg) # 目标能力：写盘前确认，失败即拒绝（不会留半套东西）。 net_err "检查回落目标 $thost:$tport（TLS 1.3 + h2 + 证书覆盖 $sni）…" net_a5_check_target "$thost" "$tport" "$sni" || return 1 net_a5_limits || return 1 # 检查与写盘之间不允许别人插进来。 net_lock a5 || return 1 net_a5_conflict || return 1 if net_port_busy tcp "$port"; then net_err "TCP $port 已被占用：" net_port_lines tcp "$port" | cut -c1-160 >&2 net_die '换端口，或先确认并停止占用方。' return 1 fi printf '即将安装 Xray 服务端（VLESS + TCP + REALITY，systemd）：\n' printf ' 版本 : v%s（官方发布，安装前校验 SHA-256）\n' "$NET_A5_VERSION" printf ' 监听 : :%s/tcp（所有地址，IPv4+IPv6）\n' "$port" printf ' 回落 : %s:%s（已验 TLS 1.3 / h2 / 证书覆盖 %s）\n' "$thost" "$tport" "$sni" printf ' 回退限速: 未认证流量 %s MiB 后限 %s KiB/s（下行）/ %s KiB/s（上行），认证连接不限\n' \ "$(( A5_AFTER / 1048576 ))" "$(( A5_DOWN_BPS / 1024 ))" "$(( A5_UP_BPS / 1024 ))" printf ' 单元 : %s\n' "$unit" printf ' 凭据 : 随机 UUID，只显示一次；私钥只写 %s\n' "$cfg" printf ' 防火墙 : 不改动；需自行放行 TCP %s\n' "$port" net_confirm_twice XRAY || return 1 tmp=$(mktemp -d) || { net_die '无法创建临时目录'; return 1; } net_err '--- 开始写入 ---' if [[ -n ${TOOLBOX_A5_ZIP_SRC:-} ]]; then cp -- "$TOOLBOX_A5_ZIP_SRC" "$tmp/xray.zip" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_die '复制本地压缩包失败'; return 1; } else net_err "下载官方发布文件：$(net_a5_zip_url "$arch")" curl -fsSL --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 --connect-timeout 15 --max-time 300 \ -o "$tmp/xray.zip" "$(net_a5_zip_url "$arch")" \ || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_die '下载失败（网络或上游不可达）。'; return 1; } fi local actual; actual=$(sha256sum -- "$tmp/xray.zip" | cut -d' ' -f1) [[ $actual == "$(net_a5_sha256 "$arch")" ]] \ || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_die "SHA-256 不匹配：期望 $(net_a5_sha256 "$arch")，实际 $actual。"; return 1; } mkdir -p "$tmp/x" && unzip -oq "$tmp/xray.zip" xray -d "$tmp/x" \ || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_die '解压失败'; return 1; } [[ -s $tmp/x/xray ]] || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_die '压缩包内没有 xray 二进制。'; return 1; } # 凭据用刚校验过的二进制自己生成，避免编码差异。 uuid=$("$tmp/x/xray" uuid 2>/dev/null) || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_die 'uuid 生成失败'; return 1; } net_valid_uuid "$uuid" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_die 'uuid 输出异常'; return 1; } # 一次调用同时取公私钥：分两次调用会拿到两对不匹配的密钥。 local kpair kpair=$("$tmp/x/xray" x25519 2>/dev/null) || kpair='' priv=$(sed -n 's/^PrivateKey: //p' <<< "$kpair") pub=$(sed -n 's/^Password (PublicKey): //p' <<< "$kpair") [[ -n $priv && -n $pub ]] || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_die 'x25519 密钥生成失败'; return 1; } shortid=$(openssl rand -hex 8) if [[ -d $etc ]]; then net_a5_etc_created=0; else install -d -m 0755 -- "$etc" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_die '无法创建配置目录'; return 1; } net_a5_etc_created=1 fi install -d -m 0755 -- "$(dirname -- "$bin")" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_die '无法创建二进制目录'; return 1; } # 配置先落在临时目录，自检通过后才到位：partial 写入不会污染 etc。 cat > "$tmp/config.json" <"$tmp/t.log" 2>&1; then net_err '配置自检失败，xray 输出：' sed -n '1,12p' "$tmp/t.log" >&2 rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a5_rollback; return 1 fi cat > "$tmp/unit" < "$smcfg" : > "$log" "$bin" run -c "$smcfg" >"$log" 2>&1 9>&- & spid=$! if ! net_a5_wait_listen "$log"; then net_err '冒烟启动失败（未监听），服务输出：' sed -n '1,12p' "$log" >&2 kill "$spid" 2>/dev/null; wait "$spid" 2>/dev/null || true rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a5_rollback; return 1 fi accepted=$(grep -c "accepted tcp:" "$log" 2>/dev/null || true) rejected=$(grep -c "invalid request user id" "$log" 2>/dev/null || true) if ! net_a5_probe "$bin" "$port" "$uuid" "$pub" "$shortid" "$sni" good; then net_err '认证探测失败（正确 UUID 未连通或无数据面）。' sed -n '1,12p' "$log" >&2 kill "$spid" 2>/dev/null; wait "$spid" 2>/dev/null || true rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a5_rollback; return 1 fi after_accepted=$(grep -c "accepted tcp:" "$log" 2>/dev/null || true) if (( after_accepted <= accepted )); then net_err '隧道有数据，但服务端日志没有认证通过记录（可能只是回退转发，认证未生效）。' sed -n '1,12p' "$log" >&2 kill "$spid" 2>/dev/null; wait "$spid" 2>/dev/null || true rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a5_rollback; return 1 fi # 负向：错误 UUID 必须被服务端明确拒绝。 net_a5_probe "$bin" "$port" '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111' \ "$pub" "$shortid" "$sni" bad || true after_rejected=$(grep -c "invalid request user id" "$log" 2>/dev/null || true) if (( after_rejected <= rejected )); then net_err '错误 UUID 没有被拒绝，认证未生效。' sed -n '1,12p' "$log" >&2 kill "$spid" 2>/dev/null; wait "$spid" 2>/dev/null || true rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a5_rollback; return 1 fi printf ' 负向探测：错误 UUID 被拒绝 ✓\n' kill "$spid" 2>/dev/null; wait "$spid" 2>/dev/null || true if ! systemctl daemon-reload; then rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a5_rollback; return 1; fi if ! systemctl enable --now "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME" >/dev/null 2>&1; then net_err 'systemctl enable --now 失败。' rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a5_rollback; return 1 fi if ! net_wait_active "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME"; then net_err '服务未进入 active 状态。' systemctl --no-pager -l status "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME" >&2 2>&1 || true rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a5_rollback; return 1 fi if ! net_wait_owned tcp "$port" "$bin" "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME"; then net_err "单元显示运行中，但 TCP $port 的监听进程不是 $NET_A5_BIN_NAME。" rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a5_rollback; return 1 fi # 真实服务上再验一次（冒烟进程已退出，这里是 systemd 起的那个）。 if ! net_a5_probe "$bin" "$port" "$uuid" "$pub" "$shortid" "$sni" live; then net_err '对已启用的服务再探测失败。' rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a5_rollback; return 1 fi printf '%s|%s|%s|%s|%s|%s|%s|%s\n' "$thost" "$tport" "$sni" "$port" "$uuid" "$pub" \ "$shortid" "$NET_A5_VERSION" > "$tmp/meta" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a5_rollback; net_die 'meta 写入失败'; return 1; } net_a5_place 0600 "$tmp/meta" "$(net_a5_meta)" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; net_a5_rollback; return 1; } rm -rf -- "$tmp" net_a5_report "$thost" "$tport" "$sni" "$port" "$uuid" "$pub" "$shortid" \ "$A5_AFTER" "$A5_DOWN_BPS" "$A5_UP_BPS" } net_a5_report() { local thost=$1 tport=$2 sni=$3 port=$4 uuid=$5 pub=$6 sid=$7 after=$8 dbps=$9 ubps=${10} printf '\n安装完成，服务 %s 已设为开机自启。\n' "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME" printf '客户端连接信息（只显示这一次，也写入 %s）：\n' "$(net_a5_meta)" printf ' vless://%s@%s:%s?type=tcp&security=reality&sni=%s&fp=chrome&pbk=%s&sid=%s&flow=xtls-rprx-vision#terry\n' \ "$uuid" "$sni" "$port" "$sni" "$pub" "$sid" printf '\n验证情况：\n' printf ' [已验证] 回落目标 %s:%s 支持 TLS 1.3、ALPN h2，证书覆盖 %s\n' "$thost" "$tport" "$sni" printf ' [已验证] 单元 active、TCP %s 由 %s 监听\n' "$port" "$NET_A5_BIN_NAME" printf ' [已验证] 正确 UUID 经 REALITY 建立隧道并有数据面响应；错误 UUID 被服务端拒绝\n' printf ' [已限制] 未认证回退流量：%s MiB 后限 %s KiB/s 下行、%s KiB/s 上行\n' \ "$(( after / 1048576 ))" "$(( dbps / 1024 ))" "$(( ubps / 1024 ))" printf '\n仍需人工处理：\n' printf ' - 防火墙/安全组放行 TCP %s（本模块不改防火墙）\n' "$port" printf ' - 目标证书更新无需改动；目标站点关停/换证书后需重装换目标\n' printf ' - 卸载：A5 → 2\n' } # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 卸载与状态 _net_a5_uninstall() { local unit etc bin state answer net_require_root || return 1 net_require_systemd || return 1 unit=$(net_a5_unit); etc=$(net_a5_etc); bin=$(net_a5_bin) state=$(net_unit_state "$unit" "$bin") if [[ $state == absent && ! -e $bin && ! -d $etc ]]; then net_die '没有找到本模块的安装（单元、二进制、配置都不存在）。' return 1 fi if [[ $state == foreign ]]; then net_die "$unit 不是本模块生成的，未删除；请人工确认。" return 1 fi printf '将停止并删除：\n %s\n %s\n %s\n' "$unit" "$bin" "$etc" net_confirm_once || return 1 net_lock a5 || return 1 # 只停/删我们的单元（ExecStart 指向我们的二进制）。 if [[ -e $unit ]] && grep -qF "ExecStart=$bin" "$unit" 2>/dev/null; then systemctl disable --now "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true rm -f -- "$unit" fi systemctl daemon-reload >/dev/null 2>&1 || true systemctl reset-failed "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true rm -f -- "$bin" [[ -d $etc ]] && rm -rf -- "$etc" rm -rf -- "$(net_a5_client)" printf '已卸载。\n' } net_a5_status() { local unit meta thost tport sni port uuid pub sid version bin unit=$(net_a5_unit); meta=$(net_a5_meta); bin=$(net_a5_bin) if [[ ! -e $unit ]]; then printf '未安装。\n' return 1 fi printf '单元: %s（%s）\n' "$unit" "$(net_unit_state "$unit" "$bin")" systemctl --no-pager -l status "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME" 2>&1 | sed -n '1,6p' if [[ -f $meta ]]; then IFS='|' read -r thost tport sni port uuid pub sid version < "$meta" || true printf '回落目标: %s:%s（SNI %s）\n' "${thost:-?}" "${tport:-?}" "${sni:-?}" printf '版本: %s\n' "${version:-?}" if [[ -n ${port:-} ]]; then printf 'TCP %s 监听: ' "$port" if net_port_owned_by tcp "$port" "$bin" "$NET_A5_UNIT_NAME"; then printf '由本服务持有\n' else printf '不是本服务\n' fi fi else printf '缺少 meta（目标/凭据未知）。\n' fi } # ================================================================ B23 DNS 切换 # # 只在 systemd-resolved 真正接管解析时动手：resolvectl 存在、服务 active、 # stub-resolv.conf 存在，且 /etc/resolv.conf 确实指向 stub。其他解析器 # （NetworkManager、openresolv、手写文件）一律 fail closed。 # 持久化用 resolved.conf.d drop-in + 重启 systemd-resolved；同时按默认路由链路 # 写运行期设置，覆盖 DHCP 下发。成功判据是 resolvectl status 与 query 都能过。 net_dns_dropin() { printf '%s/%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_DNS_DROPIN_DIR:-/etc/systemd/resolved.conf.d}" '99-terry-toolbox-dns.conf'; } net_dns_resolv() { printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_DNS_RESOLV_CONF:-/etc/resolv.conf}"; } net_dns_run() { printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_DNS_RESOLVE_RUN:-/run/systemd/resolve}"; } net_dns_require_resolved() { command -v resolvectl >/dev/null 2>&1 || { net_die '未找到 resolvectl，本机没有 systemd-resolved。'; return 1; } systemctl is-active --quiet systemd-resolved 2>/dev/null \ || { net_die 'systemd-resolved 未运行（容器或被禁用）。'; return 1; } [[ -e $(net_dns_run)/stub-resolv.conf ]] \ || { net_die "缺少 $(net_dns_run)/stub-resolv.conf，无法确认由 systemd-resolved 接管。"; return 1; } } net_dns_check_resolv() { local conf target conf=$(net_dns_resolv) [[ -e $conf || -L $conf ]] || { net_die "$conf 不存在。"; return 1; } [[ -L $conf ]] || { net_die "$conf 是普通文件（不是 systemd-resolved 的 stub 链接），解析权不在 resolved。"; return 1; } target=$(readlink -- "$conf") case $target in *systemd/resolve/stub-resolv.conf|*systemd/resolve/resolv.conf) return 0 ;; *) net_die "$conf 指向 $target，不是 systemd-resolved 的 stub，被其他程序接管。" ;; esac } # 默认路由链路。新版 systemd 直接给 "Default Route: yes" 字段，用它最准确； # 旧版只能看 Protocols 里的 +DefaultRoute，此时再排除 Current Scopes: none # （tailscale 之类会挂 +DefaultRoute 但没有 DNS）。多个或没有就返回空。 net_dns_default_link() { local out line name='' default='' count=0 scopes_none=0 has_field=0 dns_links out=$(resolvectl status 2>/dev/null) || return 0 # 该字段在 Protocol 之后才出现，先单独收集有 DNS 的链路，避免顺序依赖。 dns_links=" $(net_dns_links_with_dns | tr '\n' ' ') " grep -qE '^[[:space:]]*Default Route:' <<< "$out" && has_field=1 while IFS= read -r line; do if [[ $line =~ ^[[:space:]]*Link\ ([0-9]+)\ \((.+)\)$ ]]; then name=${BASH_REMATCH[2]}; name=${name%%@*} scopes_none=0 continue fi [[ -z $name ]] && continue # 有默认路由但没有 DNS 的链路（tailscale、docker 网桥）不算可用的出口链路。 [[ $dns_links == *" $name "* ]] || continue if [[ $line == *'Current Scopes:'* && $line == *none* ]]; then scopes_none=1 continue fi if (( has_field )); then if [[ $line =~ ^[[:space:]]*Default\ Route:[[:space:]]*yes ]]; then default=$name; ((count += 1)) fi elif [[ $line == *'+DefaultRoute'* && $scopes_none == 0 ]]; then default=$name; ((count += 1)) fi done <<< "$out" (( count == 1 )) && printf '%s\n' "$default" return 0 } # 某条链路上当前的 DNS 服务器（空格分隔，可能为空）。 net_dns_link_servers() { local line name='' out='' while IFS= read -r line; do if [[ $line =~ ^[[:space:]]*Link\ ([0-9]+)\ \((.+)\)$ ]]; then name=${BASH_REMATCH[2]}; name=${name%%@*} continue fi if [[ $name == "$1" && $line =~ ^[[:space:]]*DNS\ Servers:[[:space:]]*(.*)$ ]]; then out=${BASH_REMATCH[1]} fi done < <(resolvectl status 2>/dev/null) printf '%s' "$out" } # 部分 systemd 版本的 `resolvectl revert` 不接受空参数（会报 Interface argument # required），所以要逐条链路退回，但只碰「DNS 里含我们写入的服务器」的链路。 net_dns_revert_ours() { # servers... local our=" $* " link srv s hit while IFS= read -r link; do [[ -n $link ]] || continue srv=$(net_dns_link_servers "$link") hit=0 for s in $srv; do [[ $our == *" $s "* ]] && { hit=1; break; } done (( hit )) && resolvectl revert "$link" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true done < <(net_dns_links_with_dns) } # 配置了 DNS 服务器的链路名（一行一个），只用于判断「这条链路能不能当出口」。 net_dns_links_with_dns() { local line name='' while IFS= read -r line; do if [[ $line =~ ^[[:space:]]*Link\ ([0-9]+)\ \((.+)\)$ ]]; then name=${BASH_REMATCH[2]}; name=${name%%@*} continue fi if [[ -n $name && $line =~ ^[[:space:]]*(Current\ DNS\ Server|DNS\ Servers): ]]; then printf '%s\n' "$name" fi done < <(resolvectl status 2>/dev/null) } # 其他链路自带的 DNS：Domains=~. 下它们只在本域生效，但仍值得点名。 net_dns_leak_links() { # [跳过本链路] local line name='' found='' servers while IFS= read -r line; do if [[ $line =~ ^[[:space:]]*Link\ ([0-9]+)\ \((.+)\)$ ]]; then name=${BASH_REMATCH[2]}; name=${name%%@*} continue fi if [[ $line =~ ^[[:space:]]*(Current\ DNS\ Server|DNS\ Servers):[[:space:]]*(.*)$ ]]; then servers=${BASH_REMATCH[2]} [[ -n $name && -n $servers ]] || continue [[ -n ${1:-} && $name == "$1" ]] && continue case " $found " in *" $name "*) ;; *) found+="$name "; printf '%s(%s) ' "$name" "$servers" ;; esac fi done < <(resolvectl status 2>/dev/null) } net_dns_preset() { case $1 in 1) printf '1.1.1.1 1.0.0.1' ;; 2) printf '8.8.8.8 8.8.4.4' ;; 3) printf '9.9.9.9 149.112.112.112' ;; 4) printf '223.5.5.5 223.6.6.6' ;; 5) printf '119.29.29.29 182.254.116.116' ;; *) return 1 ;; esac } net_valid_ip() { net_valid_ipv4 "$1" || net_valid_ipv6 "$1"; } net_valid_ipv4() { local o [[ $1 =~ ^[0-9]{1,3}(\.[0-9]{1,3}){3}$ ]] || return 1 local IFS=. for o in $1; do (( o <= 255 )) || return 1; done } net_valid_ipv6() { [[ $1 == *:* && $1 =~ ^[0-9a-fA-F:.]+$ ]] || return 1 [[ $1 == *::* || $(grep -o ':' <<< "$1" | wc -l) -eq 7 ]] } net_dns_write() { # servers... local file tmp file=$(net_dns_dropin) install -d -m 0755 "$(dirname -- "$file")" || { net_die "无法创建 $(dirname -- "$file")"; return 1; } # 只保留第一次的原始备份：多次切换后回滚仍回到本工具接管前的状态。 if [[ -e $file && ! -e $file.pre-toolbox ]]; then cp -p -- "$file" "$file.pre-toolbox" || { net_die '备份现有配置失败。'; return 1; } fi tmp=$file.new.$$ { printf '# 由 Terry Toolbox B23 生成；恢复请走 B23 → 7，或删除本文件后重启 systemd-resolved。\n' printf '[Resolve]\nDNS=%s\nDomains=~.\n' "$*" } > "$tmp" || { net_die '写入临时配置失败。'; return 1; } chmod 0644 "$tmp" && mv -f -- "$tmp" "$file" \ || { rm -f -- "$tmp"; net_die '替换配置失败。'; return 1; } } net_dns_restart_resolved() { local i systemctl restart systemd-resolved >/dev/null 2>&1 || { net_die '重启 systemd-resolved 失败。'; return 1; } for ((i = 0; i < 20; i++)); do systemctl is-active --quiet systemd-resolved 2>/dev/null && return 0 sleep 0.3 done net_die 'systemd-resolved 重启后未回到 active。' } net_dns_rollback() { local file link=${1:-} file=$(net_dns_dropin) net_err '--- 开始回滚 DNS ---' if [[ -e $file.pre-toolbox ]]; then mv -f -- "$file.pre-toolbox" "$file" || net_err "警告：还原 $file 失败，请人工检查。" else rm -f -- "$file" fi [[ -n $link ]] && resolvectl revert "$link" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true systemctl restart systemd-resolved >/dev/null 2>&1 \ || net_err '警告：重启 systemd-resolved 失败，请人工确认。' net_err '已恢复原有 DNS 配置。' } # 成功判据：status 里必须能看到每个服务器，query 也必须真的能解析。 net_dns_verify() { local out s i out=$(resolvectl status 2>/dev/null) || { net_die '读取 resolvectl status 失败。'; return 1; } for s in "$@"; do grep -qF -- "$s" <<< "$out" || { net_die "resolvectl status 里看不到 $s，配置未生效。"; return 1; } done for ((i = 0; i < 3; i++)); do resolvectl query www.cloudflare.com >/dev/null 2>&1 && return 0 sleep 1 done net_die '解析查询仍然失败（服务器不可达或被拦截）。' } _net_dns_apply() { local servers=("$@") link='' s other had_backup=0 net_require_root || return 1 net_dns_require_resolved || return 1 net_dns_check_resolv || return 1 (( ${#servers[@]} > 0 )) || { net_die '没有要设置的 DNS 服务器。'; return 1; } (( ${#servers[@]} <= 2 )) || { net_die '最多两个 DNS 服务器。'; return 1; } for s in "${servers[@]}"; do net_valid_ip "$s" || { net_die "不是合法的 IPv4/IPv6 地址：$s"; return 1; } done link=$(net_dns_default_link) printf '即将修改本机 DNS：\n' for s in "${servers[@]}"; do printf ' DNS 服务器: %s\n' "$s"; done printf ' 配置文件 : %s（drop-in，重启后仍生效）\n' "$(net_dns_dropin)" if [[ -n $link ]]; then printf ' 作用链路 : %s（默认路由；全局与链路同时写，覆盖 DHCP 下发）\n' "$link" else printf ' 作用链路 : 未能唯一定位默认路由，只写全局（链路自带 DNS 可能优先）\n' fi printf ' 影响 : 会重启 systemd-resolved，DNS 可能短暂中断；明文 DNS（非 DoT/DoH）\n' net_confirm_once || return 1 net_lock dns || return 1 [[ -e $(net_dns_dropin).pre-toolbox ]] && had_backup=1 if ! net_dns_write "${servers[@]}"; then # 写失败时只在本次新建了备份的情况下清掉，避免丢掉更早的原始备份。 (( had_backup )) || rm -f -- "$(net_dns_dropin).pre-toolbox" return 1 fi if ! net_dns_restart_resolved; then net_dns_rollback "$link" return 1 fi if [[ -n $link ]]; then resolvectl dns "$link" "${servers[@]}" >/dev/null 2>&1 \ || { net_err "resolvectl dns $link 失败。"; net_dns_rollback "$link"; return 1; } resolvectl domain "$link" '~.' >/dev/null 2>&1 \ || { net_err "resolvectl domain $link 失败。"; net_dns_rollback "$link"; return 1; } fi if ! net_dns_verify "${servers[@]}"; then net_dns_rollback "$link" return 1 fi printf '\nDNS 已切换并验证通过：\n' for s in "${servers[@]}"; do printf ' [已验证] %s 出现在 resolvectl status\n' "$s"; done printf ' [已验证] resolvectl query 可以正常解析\n' printf '\n剩余泄漏面（本模块不处理，需自行评估）：\n' other=$(net_dns_leak_links "$link") if [[ -n $other ]]; then printf ' - 其他链路仍有自己的 DNS，命中其域名的查询可能走它们：%s\n' "$other" else printf ' - 当前没发现其他链路自带 DNS；以后插网卡/VPN 仍可能新增\n' fi printf ' - 明文 DNS，可被中间人看到；需要加密请另配 DNS over TLS\n' printf ' - 容器、自带解析器的应用（docker、nginx 缓存、部分 VPN 客户端）不受本设置约束\n' printf ' - 恢复原状：B23 → 7（删除 %s 并重启 systemd-resolved）\n' "$(net_dns_dropin)" } net_dns_status() { local file other file=$(net_dns_dropin) printf 'systemd-resolved: ' if systemctl is-active --quiet systemd-resolved 2>/dev/null; then printf '运行中\n'; else printf '未运行\n'; fi printf '%s -> %s\n' "$(net_dns_resolv)" \ "$(readlink -- "$(net_dns_resolv)" 2>/dev/null || printf '（普通文件）')" if [[ -e $file ]]; then printf '本工具配置: %s\n' "$file" sed -n '1,10p' "$file" else printf '本工具配置: 无（当前 DNS 不是本工具设置的）\n' fi printf '\n当前解析状态：\n' resolvectl status 2>/dev/null | sed -n '1,25p' other=$(net_dns_leak_links) [[ -n $other ]] && printf '\n其他链路 DNS（潜在泄漏）：%s\n' "$other" return 0 } _net_dns_restore() { local file ours='' net_require_root || return 1 net_dns_require_resolved || return 1 file=$(net_dns_dropin) if [[ ! -e $file && ! -e $file.pre-toolbox ]]; then net_die '没有本工具写入的 DNS 配置，无需恢复。' return 1 fi # 恢复过备份后文件还在，但已经不含本工具的标记；再恢复一次就会误删别人的配置。 if [[ -e $file && ! -e $file.pre-toolbox ]] \ && ! grep -qF '由 Terry Toolbox B23 生成' "$file" 2>/dev/null; then net_die "$file 已不是本工具生成的配置，未删除；请人工确认。" return 1 fi printf '将删除 %s 并重启 systemd-resolved（DNS 可能短暂中断）。\n' "$file" net_confirm_once || return 1 ours=$(sed -n 's/^DNS=//p' "$file" 2>/dev/null) || ours='' if [[ -e $file.pre-toolbox ]]; then mv -f -- "$file.pre-toolbox" "$file" || { net_die '还原备份失败。'; return 1; } else rm -f -- "$file" fi # shellcheck disable=SC2086 net_dns_revert_ours $ours net_dns_restart_resolved || return 1 printf '已恢复。当前解析状态：\n' resolvectl status 2>/dev/null | sed -n '1,15p' } # B23 入口：直接调用时接受 install/restore/status，不带动作时进预置菜单。 toolbox_network_dns() { local c='' input s local -a servers=() if (( $# > 0 )); then case $1 in install) shift (( $# > 0 )) || { net_die '用法: toolbox_network B23 install [DNS2]'; return 1; } ( set -e; _net_dns_apply "$@" ) ;; restore) ( set -e; _net_dns_restore ) ;; status) net_dns_status ;; *) net_die '用法: toolbox_network B23 [install [DNS2] | restore | status]' ;; esac return $? fi printf 'B23 DNS 切换（仅 systemd-resolved）\n' >&2 printf ' 1 Cloudflare 1.1.1.1/1.0.0.1\n 2 Google 8.8.8.8/8.8.4.4\n' >&2 printf ' 3 Quad9 9.9.9.9/149.112.112.112\n 4 阿里 223.5.5.5/223.6.6.6\n' >&2 printf ' 5 腾讯 119.29.29.29/182.254.116.116\n 6 自定义\n 7 恢复原状\n 8 查看状态\n请输入编号：' >&2 IFS= read -r c || return 1 case $c in 1|2|3|4|5) for s in $(net_dns_preset "$c"); do servers+=("$s"); done ( set -e; _net_dns_apply "${servers[@]}" ) ;; 6) printf '输入 1-2 个 IPv4/IPv6 地址（空格或逗号分隔）：' IFS= read -r input || return 1 input=${input//,/ } for s in $input; do servers+=("$s"); done ( set -e; _net_dns_apply "${servers[@]}" ) ;; 7) ( set -e; _net_dns_restore ) ;; 8) net_dns_status ;; *) net_die '无效选项。' ;; esac } # ================================================================ 入口 toolbox_network() { if (( $# < 1 || $# > 6 )); then printf '用法: toolbox_network \n' >&2 printf ' toolbox_network A1 [install <证书> <私钥> <主机名> [端口] | uninstall | status]\n' >&2 printf ' toolbox_network A2 [install [端口] [回落目标] | uninstall | status]\n' >&2 printf ' toolbox_network A3 [install|uninstall|status] [证书] [私钥] [主机名] [端口]\n' >&2 printf ' toolbox_network A5 [install <回落目标> [端口] | uninstall | status]\n' >&2 printf ' toolbox_network B12 [install|uninstall|status]\n' >&2 printf ' toolbox_network B23 [install | restore | status]\n' >&2 printf ' toolbox_network B31 [install|uninstall|status] <本地端口> [目标主机] [目标端口]\n' >&2 return 2 fi local key=$1 action shift || true case $key in A1) action=${1:-} if [[ -z $action ]]; then action=$(net_submenu 'A1 Alpine Hysteria 2') || return 1 set -- fi shift || true case $action in install) ( set -e; _net_a1_install "$@" ) ;; uninstall) ( set -e; net_alpine_uninstall "$NET_ALPINE_HY2_NAME" "$(net_a1_init)" \ "$(net_a1_bin)" "$(net_a1_etc)" "$(net_a1_pid)" "$(net_a1_client)" 'A1' a1 ) ;; status) net_alpine_status "$NET_ALPINE_HY2_NAME" "$(net_a1_init)" \ "$(net_a1_bin)" "$(net_a1_etc)" udp ;; *) net_die '用法: toolbox_network A1 [install <证书> <私钥> <主机名> [端口] | uninstall | status]' ;; esac return $? ;; A2) action=${1:-} if [[ -z $action ]]; then action=$(net_submenu 'A2 Alpine Xray') || return 1 set -- fi shift || true case $action in install) ( set -e; _net_a2_install "$@" ) ;; uninstall) ( set -e; net_alpine_uninstall "$NET_ALPINE_XRAY_NAME" "$(net_a2_init)" \ "$(net_a2_bin)" "$(net_a2_etc)" "$(net_a2_pid)" "$(net_a2_client)" 'A2' a2 ) ;; status) net_alpine_status "$NET_ALPINE_XRAY_NAME" "$(net_a2_init)" \ "$(net_a2_bin)" "$(net_a2_etc)" tcp ;; *) net_die '用法: toolbox_network A2 [install [端口] [回落目标] | uninstall | status]' ;; esac return $? ;; A3) action=${1:-} if [[ -z $action ]]; then action=$(net_submenu 'A3 TUIC') || return 1 set -- fi shift || true case $action in install) ( set -e; _net_tuic_install "$@" ) ;; uninstall) ( set -e; _net_tuic_uninstall ) ;; status) net_tuic_status ;; *) net_die "用法: toolbox_network A3 [install <证书> <私钥> <主机名> [端口] | uninstall | status]" ;; esac return $? ;; A4) toolbox_network_hy2 "$@"; return $? ;; A5) action=${1:-} if [[ -z $action ]]; then action=$(net_submenu 'A5 Xray (Debian)') || return 1 set -- fi shift || true case $action in install) ( set -e; _net_a5_install "$@" ) ;; uninstall) ( set -e; _net_a5_uninstall ) ;; status) net_a5_status ;; *) net_die '用法: toolbox_network A5 [install <回落目标> [端口] | uninstall | status]' ;; esac return $? ;; B12) action=${1:-} if [[ -z $action ]]; then action=$(net_submenu 'B12 TCP Brutal') || return 1 set -- fi shift || true case $action in install) ( set -e; _net_brutal_install ) ;; uninstall) ( set -e; _net_brutal_uninstall ) ;; status) net_brutal_status ;; *) net_die '用法: toolbox_network B12 [install | uninstall | status]' ;; esac return $? ;; B23) toolbox_network_dns "$@"; return $? ;; B31) action=${1:-} if [[ -z $action ]]; then action=$(net_submenu 'B31 Relay 转发') || return 1 set -- fi shift || true case $action in install) ( set -e; _net_relay_install "$@" ) ;; uninstall) ( set -e; _net_relay_uninstall "$@" ) ;; status) net_relay_status ;; *) net_die '用法: toolbox_network B31 [install <本地端口> <目标> <目标端口> | uninstall [tcp|udp 端口 | all] | status]' ;; esac return $? ;; *) printf '未知网络操作: %s\n' "$key" >&2; return 2 ;; esac } # END bundled module: network # BEGIN bundled module: system #!/usr/bin/env bash # Source-only system management module. No work is performed until toolbox_system. _toolbox_system_error() { printf '%s\n' "$*" >&2; } _toolbox_system_unimplemented() { _toolbox_system_error "$1：尚未实现；未执行任何更改。" return 1 } _toolbox_system_root() { if [[ $(id -u) != 0 ]]; then _toolbox_system_error '此操作需要 root。'; return 1; fi } _toolbox_system_confirm() { local answer printf '%s\n' "$1" >&2 printf '输入 YES 确认: ' >&2 IFS= read -r answer && [[ ${answer^^} == YES ]] || { _toolbox_system_error '已取消。'; return 1; } printf '再次输入 YES 确认: ' >&2 IFS= read -r answer && [[ ${answer^^} == YES ]] || { _toolbox_system_error '已取消。'; return 1; } } _toolbox_system_os() { # Parse OS metadata as data rather than executing shell from /etc/os-release. local detected detected=$(toolbox_network_detect_os) || return case "$detected" in alpine) printf 'alpine\n';; debian|ubuntu) printf 'debian\n';; *) _toolbox_system_error '不支持的系统'; return 1;; esac } # Package-manager and service-manager boundaries are indirected so tests can # replace them without touching the host. _toolbox_system_has() { command -v "$1" >/dev/null 2>&1; } _toolbox_system_need_tools() { local c missing=() for c in "$@"; do _toolbox_system_has "$c" || missing+=("$c"); done (( ${#missing[@]} == 0 )) || { _toolbox_system_error "缺少必需命令：${missing[*]}"; return 1; } } _toolbox_system_systemd() { _toolbox_system_has systemctl || { _toolbox_system_error '未找到 systemctl，本机不是 systemd 系统。'; return 1; } [[ -d ${TOOLBOX_SYSTEMD_RUN:-/run/systemd/system} ]] \ || { _toolbox_system_error 'systemd 未作为 init 运行（缺少 /run/systemd/system）。'; return 1; } } # Paths are overridable so isolated tests never write outside a temp root. _toolbox_system_etc() { printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_SYSTEM_ETC:-/etc/terry-toolbox}"; } _toolbox_system_state() { printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_SYSTEM_STATE:-/var/lib/terry-toolbox}"; } _toolbox_system_unit_dir() { printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_SYSTEM_UNIT_DIR:-/etc/systemd/system}"; } _toolbox_system_bin_dir() { printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_SYSTEM_BIN_DIR:-/usr/local/bin}"; } _toolbox_system_sysctl_dir(){ printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_SYSTEM_SYSCTL_DIR:-/etc/sysctl.d}"; } # Ownership markers: a file without the marker is treated as foreign and is # never overwritten, only reported. readonly _TOOLBOX_SYS_MARK_B7='# terry-toolbox B7 traffic config' readonly _TOOLBOX_SYS_MARK_B15='# terry-toolbox B15 telegram config' readonly _TOOLBOX_SYS_MARK_B16='# Managed by terry-toolbox (B16)' readonly _TOOLBOX_SYS_MARK_B19='# terry-toolbox B19 port-hop' _toolbox_system_ours() { local file=$1 marker=$2 [[ -f $file ]] && grep -qxF -- "$marker" "$file" 2>/dev/null } # Print a file atomically next to its target (same filesystem), mode 0600/0644. _toolbox_system_put() { local target=$1 mode=$2 tmp tmp=$(mktemp "${target}.tmp.XXXXXX") || return 1 cat > "$tmp" || { rm -f -- "$tmp"; return 1; } chmod "$mode" -- "$tmp" || { rm -f -- "$tmp"; return 1; } if [[ ${3:-replace} == create ]]; then ln -- "$tmp" "$target" || { rm -f -- "$tmp"; return 1; } rm -f -- "$tmp" else mv -f -- "$tmp" "$target" || { rm -f -- "$tmp"; return 1; } fi } _toolbox_system_file_stamp() { local file=$1 inode digest if [[ ! -e $file && ! -L $file ]]; then printf 'absent'; return 0; fi [[ -f $file && ! -L $file ]] || return 1 inode=$(stat -c '%d:%i' -- "$file") || return 1 digest=$(sha256sum -- "$file") || return 1 printf '%s:%s' "$inode" "${digest%% *}" } # Hidden input: value never appears in the terminal echo or in logs. _toolbox_system_read_secret() { local __value printf '%s: ' "$2" >&2 IFS= read -rs __value || { printf '\n' >&2; _toolbox_system_error "$2 未输入。"; return 1; } printf '\n' >&2 [[ -n $__value ]] || { _toolbox_system_error "$2 不能为空。"; return 1; } printf -v "$1" '%s' "$__value" } _toolbox_system_read_line() { local __value printf '%s: ' "$2" >&2 IFS= read -r __value || { _toolbox_system_error "$2 未输入。"; return 1; } [[ -n $__value ]] || { _toolbox_system_error "$2 不能为空。"; return 1; } printf -v "$1" '%s' "$__value" } _toolbox_system_positive_int() { [[ $1 =~ ^[1-9][0-9]{0,6}$ ]]; } # 0=installed, 1=absent, 2=unable to determine. No shell source/eval. _toolbox_system_package_installed() { local out rc=0 out=$(dpkg-query -W -f='${Status}' -- "$1" 2>/dev/null) || rc=$? (( rc != 1 )) || return 1 (( rc == 0 )) || return 2 case $out in 'install ok installed') return 0;; *' not-installed'|*' config-files') return 1;; *) return 2;; esac } _toolbox_system_missing_packages() { local pkg status missing_packages=() for pkg in "$@"; do status=0; _toolbox_system_package_installed "$pkg" || status=$? case $status in 0) ;; 1) missing_packages+=("$pkg");; *) _toolbox_system_error "无法确认包状态：$pkg；未安装。"; return 1;; esac done } _toolbox_system_deps() { local os local -a missing_packages=() _toolbox_system_root || return os=$(_toolbox_system_os) || return if [[ $os == debian ]]; then _toolbox_system_need_tools apt-get dpkg-query || return _toolbox_system_missing_packages curl socat wget sudo nano iptables || return if (( ${#missing_packages[@]} == 0 )); then printf '基础依赖包已齐全；未升级或安装。\n'; return 0; fi _toolbox_system_confirm "将先 apt-get update，再仅补缺失包：${missing_packages[*]}（--no-upgrade；依赖仍可能改变）。" || return apt-get update || return apt-get install -y --no-upgrade "${missing_packages[@]}" else local c local -a required_commands=(curl socat wget sudo nano iptables ss) # BusyBox wget is sufficient when present. ss is a split Alpine package: # https://pkgs.alpinelinux.org/package/v3.24/main/x86_64/iproute2-ss for c in "${required_commands[@]}"; do _toolbox_system_has "$c" && continue case $c in ss) missing_packages+=(iproute2-ss);; *) missing_packages+=("$c");; esac done if (( ${#missing_packages[@]} == 0 )); then printf '基础依赖命令已齐全；未升级或安装。\n'; return 0; fi _toolbox_system_need_tools apk || return _toolbox_system_confirm "将通过 apk add --no-cache 仅补缺失命令对应包：${missing_packages[*]}；不执行 upgrade，依赖仍可能改变。" || return apk add --no-cache "${missing_packages[@]}" || return _toolbox_system_need_tools "${required_commands[@]}" || { _toolbox_system_error '补装后命令回读失败；保留已安装包，请检查 apk 输出与 PATH。'; return 1; } printf '基础依赖命令回读通过；未执行全量升级。\n' fi } _toolbox_system_bbr() { local available current _toolbox_system_root || return available=$(sysctl -n net.ipv4.tcp_available_congestion_control) || return case " $available " in *' bbr '*) ;; *) _toolbox_system_error '内核当前未提供 BBR；未改动。'; return 1;; esac current=$(sysctl -n net.ipv4.tcp_congestion_control) || return if [[ $current == bbr ]]; then printf 'BBR 已启用。\n'; return 0; fi _toolbox_system_confirm "将当前 TCP 拥塞控制从 $current 切换到 bbr（仅当前运行期，不写持久配置）；回滚: sysctl -w net.ipv4.tcp_congestion_control=$current" || return sysctl -w net.ipv4.tcp_congestion_control=bbr || return current=$(sysctl -n net.ipv4.tcp_congestion_control) || return [[ $current == bbr ]] || { _toolbox_system_error '设置后验证失败；请检查系统状态。'; return 1; } printf 'BBR 已启用（重启后可能失效）。\n' } readonly _TOOLBOX_DOCKER_GPG_FINGERPRINT='9DC858229FC7DD38854AE2D88D81803C0EBFCD88' # Fingerprint source: HTTPS key linked by https://docs.docker.com/engine/install/debian/ # https://download.docker.com/linux/debian/gpg (same primary key on Ubuntu). # Official deb822 repository/keyring layout follows Docker's Debian/Ubuntu docs. # No convenience script, apt-key, unsigned mirror or automatic conflict removal. _toolbox_system_docker_release() { local key value id='' codename='' ubuntu_codename='' while IFS='=' read -r key value; do value=${value#\"}; value=${value%\"}; value=${value#\'}; value=${value%\'} case $key in ID) id=$value;; VERSION_CODENAME) codename=$value;; UBUNTU_CODENAME) ubuntu_codename=$value;; esac done < "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OS_RELEASE:-/etc/os-release}" || return 1 if [[ $id == ubuntu && -n $ubuntu_codename ]]; then codename=$ubuntu_codename; fi case "$id:$codename" in debian:bookworm|debian:trixie|ubuntu:jammy|ubuntu:noble|ubuntu:resolute) printf '%s %s\n' "$id" "$codename";; *) _toolbox_system_error 'Docker 官方仓库只支持已验证的 Debian/Ubuntu 发行版；未猜测代号。'; return 1;; esac } _toolbox_system_docker_verify() { local version local -a endpoint=() [[ $# == 0 ]] || endpoint=(--host "$1") _toolbox_system_has docker || { _toolbox_system_error '安装后 docker 命令仍不可用。'; return 1; } version=$(docker "${endpoint[@]}" version --format '{{.Server.Version}}' 2>/dev/null) || { _toolbox_system_error '无法回读 Docker Engine 版本（服务可能未启动）；不宣称安装成功。'; return 1; } if [[ ! $version =~ ^([0-9]{1,3})\.([0-9]+)\.([0-9]+)(\+[A-Za-z0-9._+-]+)?$ ]] \ || (( 10#${BASH_REMATCH[1]} < 28 )); then _toolbox_system_error "Docker Engine 版本 '$version' 不满足应用菜单 ≥28 门槛；未降低安全要求。"; return 1 fi printf 'Docker Engine 已安装并回读版本 %s（≥28）；未创建测试容器，应用仍需各自验收。\n' "$version" } _toolbox_system_docker_repo_cleanup() { local i target now for ((i=0; i<${#repo_paths[@]}; i++)); do [[ -n ${repo_owned[i]:-} ]] || continue target=${repo_paths[i]} now=$(stat -c '%d:%i' -- "$target" 2>/dev/null) || now='' if [[ $now == "${repo_owned[i]}" && ! -L $target ]]; then rm -f -- "$target" else _toolbox_system_error "Docker 仓库文件已变化，未清理：$target"; fi done } # APT can prefer a repository instance even when given a local .deb of the same # version. Refuse any globally indexed vendor candidate instead of trusting that # a version pin or local-file argument proves package provenance. _toolbox_system_docker_global_candidates_absent() { local pkg policy candidate count line in_table indexed for pkg in docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin; do policy=$(LC_ALL=C apt-cache policy "$pkg") || return 1 candidate=''; count=0; in_table=0; indexed=0 while IFS= read -r line; do if [[ $line =~ ^[[:space:]]*Candidate:[[:space:]]+(.+)$ ]]; then candidate=${BASH_REMATCH[1]}; count=$((count + 1)) fi if (( in_table )) && [[ $line == *[![:space:]]* ]]; then indexed=1; fi [[ $line != *'Version table:'* ]] || in_table=1 done <<< "$policy" # Unknown packages may produce no stdout; reject pinned-out versions too. if [[ -z $policy ]]; then continue; fi [[ $count == 1 && $candidate == '(none)' && $indexed == 0 ]] || { _toolbox_system_error "全局 APT 已有 $pkg 候选或状态未知；不混用现有包源，请人工处理。"; return 1; } done } # Alpine only supports a NEW local daemon in this installer. Existing/partial # installs require manual recovery; no ownership marker is fabricated on retry. _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_preflight() { local c pkg status path _toolbox_system_need_tools apk rc-service rc-status || return 1 [[ -r ${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_RUN:-/run/openrc}/softlevel ]] \ && rc-status >/dev/null 2>&1 || { _toolbox_system_error '需要已运行的 OpenRC（/run/openrc/softlevel 与 rc-status）；不在 chroot/无 init 环境安装或手工伪造状态。'; return 1; } if [[ -n ${DOCKER_HOST:-}${DOCKER_CONTEXT:-}${DOCKER_TLS_VERIFY:-}${DOCKER_CERT_PATH:-}${DOCKER_CONFIG:-}${DOCKER_OPTS:-}${DOCKERD_BINARY:-} ]]; then _toolbox_system_error '检测到 Docker 端点/CLI/daemon 环境覆盖；未安装或启动，请在无覆盖环境中人工核对。'; return 1 fi for c in docker dockerd containerd runc; do if _toolbox_system_has "$c"; then _toolbox_system_error "已有 $c；不接管现有运行时，请人工检查服务和数据。"; return 1 fi done for pkg in docker docker-engine docker-cli docker-openrc docker-cli-compose docker-cli-buildx \ docker-rootless-extras docker-systemd podman-docker containerd containerd-openrc runc; do status=0; apk info --exists "$pkg" >/dev/null 2>&1 || status=$? case $status in 1) ;; 0) _toolbox_system_error "已有包 $pkg（含可能的半安装）；未升级/启动，请人工恢复。"; return 1;; *) _toolbox_system_error "无法确认 Alpine 包状态：$pkg；未改动。"; return 1;; esac done for path in "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_DATA_ROOT:-/var/lib}/docker" "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_DATA_ROOT:-/var/lib}/containerd" \ "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_ETC_DIR:-/etc/docker}" "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_CONTAINERD_ETC_DIR:-/etc/containerd}" \ "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_INIT_DIR:-/etc/init.d}/docker" "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_INIT_DIR:-/etc/init.d}/containerd" \ "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_CONF_DIR:-/etc/conf.d}/docker" "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_CONF_DIR:-/etc/conf.d}/containerd" \ "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_RUN_DIR:-/run}/docker.sock" "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_RUN_DIR:-/run}/docker.pid" \ "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_RUN_DIR:-/run}/docker" "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_RUN_DIR:-/run}/containerd" \ "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_RUN:-/run/openrc}/started/docker" "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_RUN:-/run/openrc}/started/containerd" \ "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_RUNLEVEL_DIR:-/etc/runlevels}/"*/docker \ "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_RUNLEVEL_DIR:-/etc/runlevels}/"*/containerd; do [[ ! -e $path && ! -L $path ]] || { _toolbox_system_error "已有 Docker/containerd 配置、服务或数据路径：$path；不覆盖/接管。"; return 1; } done } _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_recovery() { _toolbox_system_error "$1；保留本次已安装包、服务文件和可能产生的数据，不自动卸载、停服或删除。" [[ -z ${install_log:-} ]] || _toolbox_system_error "安装/启动记录：$install_log（600，保留）；未声称还原安装前状态。" _toolbox_system_error '人工排查（只读）：apk info --exists docker-engine docker-openrc；rc-service docker status；日志 /var/log/docker.log。' _toolbox_system_error '核对 /etc/init.d/docker、/etc/conf.d/docker、/etc/docker、/var/lib/docker 与 /var/lib/containerd；确认归属与失败原因后才人工启动，不要再次强行安装 B1。' } _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_owned_file() { local path=$1 owner [[ -f $path && ! -L $path ]] || return 1 owner=$(apk info --who-owns "$path" 2>/dev/null) || return 1 [[ $owner == "$path is owned by docker-openrc-"* ]] } _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_logged() { local rc=0 printf '\n== %s ==\n' "$*" >> "$install_log" || return 1 "$@" >> "$install_log" 2>&1 || rc=$? return "$rc" } _toolbox_system_docker_alpine() { local service conf stamp conf_stamp i ready=0 install_log='' state _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_preflight || return 1 printf 'Alpine Docker：使用本机已配置的签名校验仓库（需启用同版本 community；不改包源、不执行 upgrade）。\n' printf ' 包：docker docker-cli-compose docker-openrc；依赖可能改变。\n' printf ' OpenRC：只启动本次新安装的 docker；依赖 sysfs/cgroups/net 也可能启动，不设置开机自启。\n' printf ' 文件/数据：包将创建 /etc/init.d/docker、/etc/conf.d/docker、/etc/containerd；daemon 可能写 /etc/docker、/var/lib/docker、/var/lib/containerd、/run/docker.sock 与 /var/log/docker.log。\n' printf ' 安装日志：%s/docker-alpine.log.*（新建随机文件，600，失败保留）。\n' "$(_toolbox_system_state)" printf ' 影响：root daemon、cgroups、网络桥与防火墙规则；不手写 daemon 配置，不接管已有服务，不改 sysctl/引导配置。\n' _toolbox_system_confirm '确认安装并通过 OpenRC 启动新的本机 Docker？回读稳定 Engine ≥28 才算成功；失败保留现场供人工恢复。' || return # Repeat absence checks after confirmation; never overwrite a new conflict. _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_preflight || return 1 state=$(_toolbox_system_state) _toolbox_system_need_tools mkdir mktemp chmod stat sha256sum || return 1 [[ ! -L $state ]] && mkdir -p -- "$state" || { _toolbox_system_error '无法安全创建安装日志目录；未安装。'; return 1; } install_log=$(mktemp "$state/docker-alpine.log.XXXXXXXX") || return 1 chmod 0600 -- "$install_log" || { _toolbox_system_error "安装日志权限设置失败，未安装：$install_log"; return 1; } printf '安装/启动记录：%s（600，保留；包与服务命令输出写入此文件）。\n' "$install_log" _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_logged apk update || { _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_recovery 'Alpine 仓库索引刷新失败，未安装 Docker'; return 1; } _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_preflight || return 1 _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_logged apk add --no-cache docker docker-cli-compose docker-openrc || { _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_recovery 'Alpine Docker 包安装失败，可能部分安装'; return 1; } service="${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_INIT_DIR:-/etc/init.d}/docker" conf="${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_CONF_DIR:-/etc/conf.d}/docker" if ! _toolbox_system_need_tools docker dockerd \ || ! apk info --exists docker-engine docker-cli docker-openrc docker-cli-compose >/dev/null 2>&1 \ || ! _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_owned_file "$service" \ || ! _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_owned_file "$conf"; then _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_recovery '安装后包/命令/OpenRC 服务文件归属未通过检查，未启动'; return 1 fi stamp=$(_toolbox_system_file_stamp "$service") && conf_stamp=$(_toolbox_system_file_stamp "$conf") || { _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_recovery '无法记录新服务文件身份，未启动'; return 1; } local path for path in "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_ETC_DIR:-/etc/docker}" \ "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_DATA_ROOT:-/var/lib}/docker" "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_DATA_ROOT:-/var/lib}/containerd" \ "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_RUN_DIR:-/run}/docker.sock" "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_RUN_DIR:-/run}/docker.pid" \ "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_RUN_DIR:-/run}/docker" "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_RUN_DIR:-/run}/containerd" \ "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_RUN:-/run/openrc}/started/docker" "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_OPENRC_RUN:-/run/openrc}/started/containerd"; do [[ ! -e $path && ! -L $path ]] || { _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_recovery "安装后出现配置/数据/运行时冲突：$path，未启动"; return 1; } done [[ $(_toolbox_system_file_stamp "$service") == "$stamp" \ && $(_toolbox_system_file_stamp "$conf") == "$conf_stamp" ]] || { _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_recovery '启动前配置已变化，未启动'; return 1; } _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_logged rc-service docker start || { _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_recovery 'OpenRC docker start 失败（不代表 daemon 一定未运行）'; return 1; } # OpenRC start returning zero is not Engine readiness. Poll only read-only # status and the explicit local socket; never restart to hide a failed start. for ((i=0; i<30; i++)); do if _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_logged rc-service docker status \ && _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_logged docker --host unix:///var/run/docker.sock version --format '{{.Server.Version}}'; then ready=1; break fi if (( i < 29 )); then sleep 1 || return 1; fi done if (( ready == 0 )) || ! _toolbox_system_docker_verify unix:///var/run/docker.sock; then _toolbox_system_docker_alpine_recovery '未通过 OpenRC/本机稳定 Engine ≥28 验收'; return 1 fi printf 'OpenRC docker 已启动并验收；不设置开机自启，未创建测试容器。若需自启，请核对后自行 rc-update add docker default。\n' } _toolbox_system_docker() { local os release distro codename arch pkg status aptroot key source tmp fingerprint gpgout local -a missing_packages=() repo_paths=() repo_owned=() _toolbox_system_root || return if _toolbox_system_has docker; then _toolbox_system_error 'Docker 已存在；为保护现有服务和数据不进行安装或重配置。需要 Engine ≥28。'; return 1 fi os=$(_toolbox_system_os) || return if [[ $os == alpine ]]; then _toolbox_system_docker_alpine return fi _toolbox_system_need_tools apt-get apt-cache dpkg dpkg-query dpkg-deb || return release=$(_toolbox_system_docker_release) || return read -r distro codename <<< "$release" arch=$(dpkg --print-architecture) || return case "$distro:$arch" in debian:amd64|debian:arm64|debian:armhf|debian:ppc64el|ubuntu:amd64|ubuntu:arm64|ubuntu:armhf|ubuntu:s390x|ubuntu:ppc64el) ;; *) _toolbox_system_error "Docker 官方仓库未验证架构 $distro:$arch；未改动。"; return 1;; esac for pkg in docker.io docker-compose docker-compose-v2 docker-doc docker-buildx podman-docker containerd runc \ docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin; do status=0; _toolbox_system_package_installed "$pkg" || status=$? case $status in 1) ;; 0) _toolbox_system_error "已有 Docker/冲突包 $pkg；不自动删除、升级或重配置，请人工迁移。"; return 1;; *) _toolbox_system_error "无法确认包状态 $pkg；未改动。"; return 1;; esac done for tmp in "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_DATA_ROOT:-/var/lib}/docker" "${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_DATA_ROOT:-/var/lib}/containerd"; do [[ ! -e $tmp && ! -L $tmp ]] || { _toolbox_system_error "已有数据路径 $tmp；不覆盖，请人工确认。"; return 1; } done aptroot=${TOOLBOX_DOCKER_APT_ROOT:-/etc/apt} key="$aptroot/keyrings/terry-toolbox-docker.asc" source="$aptroot/sources.list.d/terry-toolbox-docker.sources" for tmp in "$key" "$source" "$aptroot/keyrings/docker.asc" "$aptroot/sources.list.d/docker.sources" "$aptroot/sources.list.d/docker.list"; do [[ ! -e $tmp && ! -L $tmp ]] || { _toolbox_system_error "Docker 仓库目标已存在：$tmp；不覆盖。"; return 1; } done _toolbox_system_missing_packages ca-certificates curl gnupg || return printf '将添加 Docker 官方 stable 仓库 https://download.docker.com/linux/%s（%s/%s）。\n' "$distro" "$codename" "$arch" printf ' GPG: %s（固定主密钥指纹 %s）\n 仓库: %s（Signed-By 专用 keyring）\n' "$key" "$_TOOLBOX_DOCKER_GPG_FINGERPRINT" "$source" printf ' 包: docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin；私有索引只从官方源下载，再安装本地包（--no-upgrade）。\n' printf ' 若全局 APT 已有上述包的索引则拒绝，避免同版本镜像替换；不装 recommends，普通依赖仍来自现有系统仓库。\n' _toolbox_system_confirm '将先 update，补仓库依赖，再 update/安装 Docker。包安装可能启动服务、改变防火墙；不会删除现有容器数据。确认？' || return apt-get update || return _toolbox_system_docker_global_candidates_absent || return 1 if (( ${#missing_packages[@]} > 0 )); then apt-get install -y --no-upgrade "${missing_packages[@]}" || return fi _toolbox_system_need_tools curl gpg || return tmp=$(mktemp -d) || return mkdir -m 0700 "$tmp/gnupg" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } if ! curl -fsSL --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 --connect-timeout 15 --max-time 60 \ -o "$tmp/key.asc" "https://download.docker.com/linux/$distro/gpg"; then _toolbox_system_error 'Docker 官方 GPG 密钥下载失败；未添加仓库。'; rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1 fi if ! gpgout=$(gpg --homedir "$tmp/gnupg" --batch --with-colons --show-keys "$tmp/key.asc" 2>/dev/null); then _toolbox_system_error 'Docker GPG 密钥解析失败；未添加仓库。'; rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1 fi fingerprint=$(printf '%s\n' "$gpgout" | awk -F: '$1=="pub"{pubs++; primary=1; next} primary && $1=="fpr"{print $10; primary=0} END{if(pubs!=1)exit 1}') || fingerprint='' if [[ $fingerprint != "$_TOOLBOX_DOCKER_GPG_FINGERPRINT" ]]; then _toolbox_system_error 'Docker GPG 主密钥指纹不匹配；未添加仓库。'; rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1 fi chmod 0644 "$tmp/key.asc" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } { printf '# terry-toolbox B1 Docker official repository\nTypes: deb\nURIs: https://download.docker.com/linux/%s\n' "$distro" printf 'Suites: %s\nComponents: stable\nArchitectures: %s\nSigned-By: %s\n' "$codename" "$arch" "$key" } > "$tmp/docker.sources" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } chmod 0644 "$tmp/docker.sources" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } install -d -m 0755 "$aptroot/keyrings" "$aptroot/sources.list.d" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } repo_paths=("$key" "$source") local i staged for ((i=0; i<2; i++)); do if (( i == 0 )); then pkg="$tmp/key.asc"; else pkg="$tmp/docker.sources"; fi staged=$(mktemp "${repo_paths[i]}.tmp.XXXXXX") || { _toolbox_system_docker_repo_cleanup; rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } if ! cp -p -- "$pkg" "$staged"; then rm -f -- "$staged"; _toolbox_system_docker_repo_cleanup; rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1 fi repo_owned[i]=$(stat -c '%d:%i' -- "$staged") || { rm -f -- "$staged"; _toolbox_system_docker_repo_cleanup; rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } ln -- "$staged" "${repo_paths[i]}" || { repo_owned[i]=''; rm -f -- "$staged"; _toolbox_system_docker_repo_cleanup; rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } rm -f -- "$staged" || { _toolbox_system_docker_repo_cleanup; rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } done # A key on one source does not constrain global APT resolution. Download the # five vendor packages with ONLY our source and a fresh, private index/cache; # then install those exact local archives, resolving ordinary dependencies # through the host's repositories. Never select vendor packages globally. local -a vendor_packages=(docker-ce docker-ce-cli containerd.io docker-buildx-plugin docker-compose-plugin) local -a vendor_debs=() private_apt=() local deb metadata name version package_arch found mkdir -p "$tmp/lists/partial" "$tmp/archives/partial" "$tmp/debs" || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } private_apt=(-o "Dir::Etc::sourcelist=$source" -o 'Dir::Etc::sourceparts=-' -o "Dir::State::lists=$tmp/lists" -o "Dir::Cache::archives=$tmp/archives" -o 'Dir::Cache::pkgcache=' -o 'Dir::Cache::srcpkgcache=' -o 'Dir::Etc::preferences=-' -o 'Dir::Etc::preferencesparts=-' -o 'APT::Get::AllowUnauthenticated=false' -o 'Acquire::AllowInsecureRepositories=false') if ! apt-get "${private_apt[@]}" update; then _toolbox_system_error 'Docker 专用仓库 update 失败；只移除本轮创建的仓库文件。' _toolbox_system_docker_repo_cleanup; rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1 fi if ! ( cd -- "$tmp/debs" && apt-get "${private_apt[@]}" download "${vendor_packages[@]}" ); then _toolbox_system_error 'Docker 官方包下载失败；未安装 Docker，保留仓库供修复。' rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1 fi for pkg in "${vendor_packages[@]}"; do found=0 for deb in "$tmp/debs/"*.deb; do [[ -f $deb && ! -L $deb ]] || continue metadata=$(dpkg-deb --show --showformat='${Package} ${Version} ${Architecture}' "$deb") || { rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } read -r name version package_arch <<< "$metadata" [[ $name == "$pkg" ]] || continue [[ $package_arch == "$arch" && -n $version ]] || { _toolbox_system_error 'Docker 包架构/版本不符，未安装。'; rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } vendor_debs+=("$deb"); found=$((found + 1)) done (( found == 1 )) || { _toolbox_system_error "Docker 官方包缺失或重复：$pkg；未安装。"; rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1; } done if ! _toolbox_system_docker_global_candidates_absent; then rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1 fi if ! apt-get install -y --no-upgrade --no-install-recommends "${vendor_debs[@]}"; then _toolbox_system_error "Docker 安装失败，包可能部分安装；保留官方仓库供修复：$source。未自动卸载。" rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1 fi rm -rf -- "$tmp" _toolbox_system_docker_verify || { _toolbox_system_error '包安装/仓库保留，但未通过 Engine 验收；请人工检查服务和包状态。'; return 1; } } _toolbox_system_password() { _toolbox_system_root || return _toolbox_system_confirm '将交由 passwd 交互式修改 root 密码。请确保保留独立登录方式。' || return passwd root } _toolbox_system_reboot() { _toolbox_system_root || return _toolbox_system_confirm '将立即重启本机，中断所有服务和连接。' || return reboot } # ---------------------------------------------------------------- B2 NodeQuality # 上游没有版本号，只按 main 分支发布；脚本本体因此固定到提交号，摘要写死在代码里。 # raw.githubusercontent 对完整提交号是不可变地址：上游更新后摘要不变，但需要人改这里 # 的两行常量才会跟随（不自动更新）。摘要只证明下载内容与该提交一致，不是安全审计。 readonly _TOOLBOX_NODEQUALITY_COMMIT='a92fca6c0067df29ddd03fdc2fee6f3000f64545' readonly _TOOLBOX_NODEQUALITY_URL=\ "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/LloydAsp/NodeQuality/$_TOOLBOX_NODEQUALITY_COMMIT/NodeQuality.sh" readonly _TOOLBOX_NODEQUALITY_SHA256='4e1b25894cadf908ef61fb0d9ce874a75524c6dafc2ea26f0477107288e0c018' # 地址与摘要均可被环境变量覆盖：离线测试用，也可在上游更新后临时对照实际摘要。 _toolbox_system_nodequality_url() { printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_NODEQUALITY_URL:-$_TOOLBOX_NODEQUALITY_URL}"; } _toolbox_system_nodequality_sha() { printf '%s\n' "${TOOLBOX_NODEQUALITY_SHA256:-$_TOOLBOX_NODEQUALITY_SHA256}"; } # 上游 post_cleanup 固定以 exit 1 结束，成功与否要看输出是否走到上传完成。 _toolbox_system_nodequality() { local tmp script actual expected rc=0 left failed=0 _toolbox_system_root || return _toolbox_system_need_tools bash curl sha256sum tar mount chroot || return printf 'NodeQuality：官方综合测试（不安装软件，只在临时系统内跑一遍）\n' printf ' 脚本地址 : %s\n' "$(_toolbox_system_nodequality_url)" printf ' 固定提交 : %s\n' "$_TOOLBOX_NODEQUALITY_COMMIT" printf ' 校验 : 下载后比对固定 SHA-256 %s…（不匹配即中止，不执行）\n' \ "$(_toolbox_system_nodequality_sha | cut -c1-12)" printf ' 依据 : 摘要只证明文件与该提交一致，不等于安全审计；上游更新需改代码里的常量\n' printf ' 运行方式 : 在临时目录放脚本，chroot 挂载 BenchOs，测完自动卸载并删除该目录\n' printf ' 仍在变动 : BenchOS（release v0.0.2）与 main 分支的 part/*.sh、各检查脚本每次运行现下载\n' printf ' 上传 : 结果 zip 会上传到 api.nodequality.com（第三方接口，可能失败）\n' printf ' 退出码 : 上游结尾固定 exit 1，返回 1 属正常；以输出是否走到上传完成为准\n' printf ' 影响面 : 需要 root；运行期短暂挂载 proc/sys/dev，结束后卸载；不改系统配置\n' _toolbox_system_confirm '确认下载固定版本脚本，校验通过后在临时目录执行？' || return tmp=$(mktemp -d) || return 1 script="$tmp/NodeQuality.sh" if ! curl -fsSL --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 --connect-timeout 15 --max-time 60 \ -o "$script" "$(_toolbox_system_nodequality_url)"; then _toolbox_system_error '下载脚本失败；未执行。' rm -rf -- "$tmp"; return 1 fi actual=$(sha256sum -- "$script" | cut -d' ' -f1) expected=$(_toolbox_system_nodequality_sha) if [[ $actual != "$expected" ]]; then _toolbox_system_error "脚本 SHA-256 不匹配：期望 $expected，实际 $actual。未执行。" _toolbox_system_error "上游可能已更新；核对无误后可临时用 TOOLBOX_NODEQUALITY_SHA256=$actual 复跑。" _toolbox_system_error "已保留下载文件供人工审阅：$script" return 1 fi printf '校验通过（%s…）。开始执行，以下输出直接来自上游脚本，中途会提问。\n\n' "${actual:0:12}" # 上游以当前目录为基准建 .nodequality<时间戳> 工作目录，故必须先进临时目录。 ( cd "$tmp" && bash ./NodeQuality.sh ) || rc=$? if (( rc != 0 && rc != 1 )); then _toolbox_system_error "上游脚本异常退出（$rc）；可能只跑了一部分。" failed=1 fi # 正常收尾会删掉工作目录；残留说明卸载或清理没成功，保留临时目录供检查。 left=$(find "$tmp" -maxdepth 1 -name '.nodequality*' -print -quit 2>/dev/null) if [[ -n $left ]]; then _toolbox_system_error "检测到未清理的工作目录 $left：可能挂载残留，重启后确认再删除。" _toolbox_system_error "临时目录保留在 $tmp（含 error.log，若有）。" return 1 fi rm -rf -- "$tmp" if (( failed )); then return 1; fi printf '\nNodeQuality 已结束：结果已尝试上传，临时目录已删除，本机配置未改动。\n' printf '若上传失败，输出里会显示上传接口报错，需重跑；屏幕上的报告不会自动保存。\n' } _toolbox_system_trace() { local destination label failed=0 _toolbox_system_has traceroute || { _toolbox_system_error '缺少 traceroute；请先手动安装。'; return 1; } # Published example targets, not an assurance of current carrier ownership: # https://github.com/zhucaidan/mtr_trace (README, Beijing destinations). _toolbox_system_confirm '将发送 UDP traceroute 探测到北京电信 219.141.136.12、联通 202.106.50.1、移动 221.179.155.161；目标可能变更或不响应。输出是本机至目标的 IPv4 路由，不自动判断线路。' || return while IFS=' ' read -r label destination; do printf '\n=== %s (%s) ===\n' "$label" "$destination" if ! traceroute -n -m 20 -w 2 -q 1 "$destination"; then _toolbox_system_error "$label 探测命令失败。" failed=1 fi done <<'TARGETS' 北京电信 219.141.136.12 北京联通 202.106.50.1 北京移动 221.179.155.161 TARGETS printf '\n提示：命令成功不代表目标可达；星号可能是丢包或设备不响应。\n' (( failed == 0 )) } # ---------------------------------------------------------------- B7 流量统计 # vnstat 只读取内核计数，不进入转发路径，也不修改防火墙；因此这里只做统计和 # 阈值告警，不在超额时阻断网络。阈值告警由 systemd timer 每小时调用一次， # 同一自然日最多告警一次，避免刷屏。 _TOOLBOX_TRAFFIC_CHECK='terry-toolbox-traffic-check' _TOOLBOX_TRAFFIC_SERVICE='terry-toolbox-traffic.service' _TOOLBOX_TRAFFIC_TIMER='terry-toolbox-traffic.timer' _toolbox_system_vnstat_json_ok() { local out out=$(vnstat --json 2>/dev/null) || return 1 # vnstat exits 0 before the database exists but emits no JSON object. [[ $out == *'"interfaces"'* ]] } # Shared B7/B15 transaction: preflight every target, keep backups of ALL existing # artifacts, publish only if identity/content still match, rollback only our writes. _toolbox_system_unit_guard() { local target=$1 name=${1##*/} fragment drops fragment=$(systemctl show -p FragmentPath --value "$name" 2>/dev/null) || { _toolbox_system_error "无法确认单元来源：$name；未改动。"; return 1; } drops=$(systemctl show -p DropInPaths --value "$name" 2>/dev/null) || { _toolbox_system_error "无法确认单元 drop-in：$name；未改动。"; return 1; } if [[ -n $fragment && $fragment != "$target" ]] || [[ -n ${drops//[[:space:]]/} ]] \ || [[ -e $target.d || -L $target.d ]]; then _toolbox_system_error "同名单元来源或 drop-in 不受本工具管理：$name；未改动。"; return 1 fi } _toolbox_system_unit_stopped() { local state state=$(systemctl show -p ActiveState --value "$1" 2>/dev/null) || return 1 case $state in inactive|failed) return 0;; *) return 1;; esac } _toolbox_system_timer_preflight() { local marker=$1 target shift for target in "$@"; do if [[ -e $target || -L $target ]] && { [[ -L $target ]] || ! _toolbox_system_ours "$target" "$marker"; }; then _toolbox_system_error "$target 已存在但不是本工具生成的，未覆盖；请人工确认归属。" return 1 fi done for target in "$@"; do case $target in *.service|*.timer) _toolbox_system_unit_guard "$target" || return 1;; esac done } _toolbox_system_timer_begin() { local i target active enabled _toolbox_system_timer_preflight "$txn_marker" "${txn_paths[@]}" || return 1 txn_dir=$(mktemp -d "$(_toolbox_system_state)/timer-txn.XXXXXX") || return 1 for ((i=0; i<${#txn_paths[@]}; i++)); do target=${txn_paths[i]}; txn_before[i]=''; txn_written[i]='' if [[ -e $target ]]; then cp -p -- "$target" "$txn_dir/old.$i" || return 1 txn_before[i]=$(stat -c '%d:%i' -- "$target") || return 1 txn_hash[i]=$(sha256sum -- "$target") || return 1 fi done active=$(systemctl show -p ActiveState --value "$txn_timer" 2>/dev/null) || return 1 case $active in active) txn_active=1;; inactive|failed) ;; *) _toolbox_system_error '无法确定此前定时器活动状态，未写入。'; return 1;; esac if [[ -n ${txn_before[3]} ]]; then enabled=$(systemctl show -p UnitFileState --value "$txn_timer" 2>/dev/null) || return 1 case $enabled in enabled) txn_enabled=1;; disabled) ;; *) _toolbox_system_error '无法确定此前定时器启用状态，未写入。'; return 1;; esac else enabled=$(systemctl show -p UnitFileState --value "$txn_timer" 2>/dev/null) || return 1 case $enabled in ''|disabled|not-found) ;; *) _toolbox_system_error '无受管文件但定时器已存在，未写入。'; return 1;; esac fi return 0 } _toolbox_system_timer_commit() { local i target now digest # Re-check ALL paths before the first write, including changes during confirmation. _toolbox_system_timer_preflight "$txn_marker" "${txn_paths[@]}" || return 1 for ((i=0; i<${#txn_paths[@]}; i++)); do target=${txn_paths[i]} if [[ -n ${txn_before[i]} ]]; then now=$(stat -c '%d:%i' -- "$target") || return 1 [[ $now == "${txn_before[i]}" && $(sha256sum -- "$target") == "${txn_hash[i]}" ]] \ || { _toolbox_system_error "确认后文件已变化：$target；未覆盖。"; return 1; } elif [[ -e $target || -L $target ]]; then _toolbox_system_error "目标已被其他操作创建：$target；未覆盖。"; return 1 fi done local staged identity for ((i=0; i<${#txn_paths[@]}; i++)); do target=${txn_paths[i]} staged=$(mktemp "${target}.tmp.XXXXXX") || return 1 if ! cp -p -- "$txn_dir/new.$i" "$staged"; then rm -f -- "$staged"; return 1; fi identity=$(stat -c '%d:%i' -- "$staged") || { rm -f -- "$staged"; return 1; } digest=$(sha256sum -- "$staged") || { rm -f -- "$staged"; return 1; } if [[ -z ${txn_before[i]} ]]; then # Adjacent hardlink is atomic and no-clobber even on separate /etc & /var. ln -- "$staged" "$target" || { rm -f -- "$staged"; return 1; } txn_written[i]=$identity txn_written_hash[i]=${digest%% *} rm -f -- "$staged" || return 1 else now=$(stat -c '%d:%i' -- "$target") || { rm -f -- "$staged"; return 1; } if [[ -L $target || $now != "${txn_before[i]}" || $(sha256sum -- "$target") != "${txn_hash[i]}" ]]; then rm -f -- "$staged"; _toolbox_system_error "目标在发布期间变化，未覆盖：$target"; return 1 fi mv -f -- "$staged" "$target" || { rm -f -- "$staged"; return 1; } txn_written[i]=$identity txn_written_hash[i]=${digest%% *} fi done } _toolbox_system_timer_cleanup() { local i target now digest changed=0 [[ -n $txn_dir && -d $txn_dir ]] || return 0 for now in "${txn_written[@]}"; do [[ -z $now ]] || changed=1; done if (( changed == 1 )); then # Check ALL artifacts before touching any: inode alone misses in-place edits. for ((i=0; i<${#txn_paths[@]}; i++)); do [[ -n ${txn_written[i]} ]] || continue target=${txn_paths[i]} now=$(stat -c '%d:%i' -- "$target" 2>/dev/null) || return 1 digest=$(sha256sum -- "$target" 2>/dev/null) || return 1 [[ $now == "${txn_written[i]}" && ! -L $target && ${digest%% *} == "${txn_written_hash[i]}" ]] \ || { _toolbox_system_error "文件已变化，未清理任何文件；备份：$txn_dir"; return 1; } done for target in "${txn_paths[@]}"; do case $target in *.service|*.timer) _toolbox_system_unit_guard "$target" || return 1;; esac done if ! systemctl disable --now "$txn_timer" >/dev/null 2>&1 \ || ! _toolbox_system_unit_stopped "$txn_timer" \ || ! systemctl stop "$txn_service" >/dev/null 2>&1 \ || ! _toolbox_system_unit_stopped "$txn_service"; then _toolbox_system_error "定时器停用未确认；保留全部文件和备份：$txn_dir"; return 1 fi for ((i=0; i<${#txn_paths[@]}; i++)); do [[ -n ${txn_written[i]} ]] || continue target=${txn_paths[i]} now=$(stat -c '%d:%i' -- "$target" 2>/dev/null) || now='' digest=$(sha256sum -- "$target" 2>/dev/null) || return 1 [[ $now == "${txn_written[i]}" && ! -L $target && ${digest%% *} == "${txn_written_hash[i]}" ]] \ || { _toolbox_system_error "文件已变化，未清理：$target；备份：$txn_dir"; return 1; } if [[ -f $txn_dir/old.$i ]]; then mv -f -- "$txn_dir/old.$i" "$target" || return 1 else rm -f -- "$target" || return 1 fi done systemctl daemon-reload >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 1 if (( txn_active == 1 )); then systemctl enable --now "$txn_timer" >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 1 systemctl is-active --quiet "$txn_timer" || return 1 elif (( txn_enabled == 1 )); then systemctl enable "$txn_timer" >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 1 fi fi rm -rf -- "$txn_dir" } _toolbox_system_traffic_cleanup() { _toolbox_system_timer_cleanup; } _toolbox_system_write_traffic_check() { local target=$1 etc state etc=$(_toolbox_system_etc); state=$(_toolbox_system_state) cat > "$target" </dev/null || printf '%s\n' "\$*" >&2; } [[ -r \$CONF ]] || exit 0 limit='' while IFS='=' read -r key value; do [[ \$key == LIMIT_GIB ]] && limit=\$value done < "\$CONF" case \${limit:-} in ''|*[!0-9]*) exit 0;; esac (( limit > 0 )) || exit 0 command -v vnstat >/dev/null 2>&1 || { log 'vnstat 不存在，跳过检查'; exit 0; } command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 || { log '缺少 jq，跳过检查'; exit 0; } json=\$(vnstat --json 2>/dev/null) || { log 'vnstat 读取失败'; exit 0; } bytes=\$(printf '%s' "\$json" | jq -r --arg y "\$(date +%Y)" --argjson m "\$((10#\$(date +%m)))" \\ '[ .interfaces[]? | select(.name != "lo") | .traffic.month[]? | select((.date.year|tostring) == \$y and .date.month == \$m) | ((.rx // 0) + (.tx // 0)) ] | add // 0' 2>/dev/null) || exit 0 case \${bytes:-} in ''|*[!0-9]*) exit 0;; esac used=\$(awk -v b="\$bytes" 'BEGIN { printf "%.2f", b / 1073741824 }') over=\$(awk -v u="\$used" -v l="\$limit" 'BEGIN { print (u >= l) ? 1 : 0 }') (( over == 1 )) || exit 0 mkdir -p "\$STATE" 2>/dev/null || exit 0 marker="\$STATE/traffic-\$(date +%Y%m).state" today=\$(date +%F) if [[ -r \$marker && \$(<"\$marker") == "\$today" ]]; then exit 0; fi msg="\$(hostname) 本月流量已用 \${used} GiB，达到阈值 \${limit} GiB（仅告警，未阻断）。" log "\$msg" [[ -r \$TG_CONF ]] || exit 0 token=''; chat='' while IFS='=' read -r key value; do case \$key in TOKEN) token=\$value;; CHAT_ID) chat=\$value;; esac done < "\$TG_CONF" [[ -n \$token && -n \$chat ]] || exit 0 response=\$(curl -fsS --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 --connect-timeout 10 --max-time 20 \\ --data-urlencode "chat_id=\$chat" --data-urlencode "text=\$msg" \\ "https://api.telegram.org/bot\$token/sendMessage") \\ || { log 'Telegram 发送失败，未记录今日告警，稍后重试'; exit 1; } printf '%s' "\$response" | jq -e '.ok == true' >/dev/null 2>&1 \\ || { log 'Telegram 未确认成功，未记录今日告警，稍后重试'; exit 1; } [[ ! -L \$marker ]] || { log '告警标记是符号链接，未写入'; exit 1; } printf '%s\n' "\$today" > "\$marker" || { log '告警已送达但标记写入失败'; exit 1; } exit 0 EOF chmod 0755 -- "$target" } _toolbox_system_traffic() { local os etc state bin unit_dir conf limit installed=0 ready=0 i local txn_dir='' txn_marker=$_TOOLBOX_SYS_MARK_B7 txn_timer=$_TOOLBOX_TRAFFIC_TIMER txn_service=$_TOOLBOX_TRAFFIC_SERVICE txn_active=0 txn_enabled=0 local -a txn_paths=() txn_before=() txn_hash=() txn_written=() txn_written_hash=() _toolbox_system_root || return os=$(_toolbox_system_os) || return [[ $os == debian ]] || { _toolbox_system_error '流量统计目前只支持 Debian/Ubuntu 的 vnstat 包；Alpine 未验证。'; return 1; } _toolbox_system_systemd || return _toolbox_system_need_tools curl || return etc=$(_toolbox_system_etc); state=$(_toolbox_system_state) bin=$(_toolbox_system_bin_dir); unit_dir=$(_toolbox_system_unit_dir) conf="$etc/traffic.conf" txn_paths=("$conf" "$bin/$_TOOLBOX_TRAFFIC_CHECK" "$unit_dir/$_TOOLBOX_TRAFFIC_SERVICE" "$unit_dir/$_TOOLBOX_TRAFFIC_TIMER") _toolbox_system_timer_preflight "$txn_marker" "${txn_paths[@]}" || return 1 _toolbox_system_has vnstat && installed=1 _toolbox_system_read_line limit '每月告警阈值（GiB，整数；0 表示只统计不告警）' || return case $limit in 0) ;; *) _toolbox_system_positive_int "$limit" || { _toolbox_system_error "阈值不正确：$limit"; return 1; };; esac if (( installed == 1 )); then printf 'vnstat 已安装：只更新阈值和定时器，不重装包。\n' else printf 'vnstat 未安装：将从系统仓库安装 vnstat 和 jq（jq 用于解析统计 JSON）。\n' fi printf ' 阈值 : %s\n' "$( (( limit == 0 )) && printf '只统计' || printf '%s GiB/月' "$limit" )" printf ' 单位 : GiB（1 GiB = 1024^3 字节）\n' printf ' 检查 : 每小时一次，超额时同日最多告警一次\n' printf ' 通知 : 写入系统日志；若已配置 B15 则同时发 Telegram\n' printf ' 阻断 : 不阻断流量，超额后网络照常\n' _toolbox_system_confirm 'vnstat 会持续写入本机统计库；统计值来自内核计数，只统计不改变网络配置。确认继续？' || return install -d -m 0755 "$etc" "$state" "$bin" "$unit_dir" || return 1 if (( installed == 0 )); then apt-get install -y vnstat jq || { _toolbox_system_error 'vnstat/jq 安装失败；未写入配置。'; return 1 } _toolbox_system_has jq || { _toolbox_system_error 'jq 仍不可用；未写入配置。'; return 1 } systemctl enable --now vnstat >/dev/null 2>&1 || { _toolbox_system_error 'vnstat 服务启用失败；未写入配置。'; return 1 } else _toolbox_system_has jq \ || { _toolbox_system_error '已装 vnstat 但缺少 jq（检查脚本用它解析 JSON）。'; return 1; } systemctl enable --now vnstat >/dev/null 2>&1 || _toolbox_system_error '提示：vnstat 服务启用失败，统计可能不更新。' fi for ((i = 0; i < 30; i++)); do if _toolbox_system_vnstat_json_ok; then ready=1; break; fi sleep 1 done if (( ready == 0 )); then _toolbox_system_error 'vnstat 在 30 秒内未产生可用统计（数据库未就绪）；包已安装，配置未写入。' _toolbox_system_traffic_cleanup return 1 fi _toolbox_system_timer_begin || { _toolbox_system_traffic_cleanup; return 1; } { printf '%s\n' "$_TOOLBOX_SYS_MARK_B7" printf '# 由 Terry Toolbox B7 管理；值来自本工具询问。\n' printf 'LIMIT_GIB=%s\n' "$limit" } | _toolbox_system_put "$txn_dir/new.0" 0600 || { _toolbox_system_error '写入阈值配置失败。'; _toolbox_system_traffic_cleanup; return 1; } if ! _toolbox_system_write_traffic_check "$txn_dir/new.1"; then _toolbox_system_error '写入检查脚本失败。' _toolbox_system_traffic_cleanup return 1 fi cat > "$txn_dir/new.2" < "$txn_dir/new.3" </dev/null 2>&1; then _toolbox_system_error '定时器启用失败，尝试回滚本次写入。' _toolbox_system_traffic_cleanup return 1 fi if ! systemctl is-active --quiet "$_TOOLBOX_TRAFFIC_TIMER"; then _toolbox_system_error '定时器未进入 active 状态，尝试回滚本次写入。' _toolbox_system_traffic_cleanup return 1 fi rm -rf -- "$txn_dir" || return 1 if (( limit == 0 ));
